(<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/prefab-referencing-a-script-is-not-shown-in-the-search-windows-project-tab-when-using-find-references-in-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-86364</a>)

-   Editor: Ensure we do not use ADB in FindRef to avoid loading all assets. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/search-window-freezes-and-takes-a-long-time-to-open-when-using-find-references-in-project-context-menu-option" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117182</a>)

-   Editor: Exclude LODGroups with animated crossfade from using instanced rendering to ensure correct behavior. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/animated-cross-fade-on-lodgroups-doesnt-work-with-gpu-resident-drawer" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-91634</a>)

-   Editor: Executing must correctly setup a Table if it contains one. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/tableview-not-setup-properly-after-executing-search-query" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107879</a>)

-   Editor: Fix crash in GameObject::IsActiveIgnoreImplicitPrefab() (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-gameobject-isactiveignoreimplicitprefab-when-opening-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-92622</a>)

-   Editor: fix foldout name too long and not truncated (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/longer-scaler-profile-names-go-out-of-the-scaler-profilers-section" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110903</a>)

-   Editor: Fix Graphics Packages Samples dependencies getting imported after sample itself. (UUM-114222)

-   Editor: Fix MacEditor Popup windows sometimes appearing in the wrong Workspace (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/macos-add-component-button-is-broken-when-the-editor-is-moved-to-a-different-macos-workspace" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111923</a>)

-   Editor: Fix tag validation UI possible overflow (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/critical-error-icon-disappears-when-adding-long-tag-names-in-a-popup-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112449</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed "Add Build Profile" button alignment and spacing. (UUM-101668)

-   Editor: Fixed "Unity Services are not disabled and cause longer build times when the project is not connected to Unity Cloud" warning. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unity-services-are-not-disabled-and-cause-longer-build-times-when-the-project-is-not-connected-to-unity-cloud" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120502</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a BRG failing test on Android Samsung S21. (UUM-108763)

-   Editor: Fixed a BRG/GRD validation layer warning on Metal. (UUM-115852)

-   Editor: Fixed a broken documentation link in a MainToolbar warning message. (STO-3653)

-   Editor: Fixed a broken help link in the UI Toolkit Project Settings window. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-toolkit-bug-bundle-may-1-2025" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104492</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a bug causing issues with keywords in material variants to not change when reverting a property override. (UUM-109470)

-   Editor: Fixed a bug that was causing an exception in the console when asset bundles containing terrains created by an older version of Unity were loaded that contained instanced terrain details. (UUM-117177)

-   Editor: Fixed a bug where ctrl + left click would not open the contextual menu for the Orientation Gizmo and added support for Mac's two-finger click. (UUM-102749)

-   Editor: Fixed a bug where terrain with GPU instancing enabled would not render in the Scene view when using one of the debug visualization modes. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/terrain-disappears-in-debug-draw-mode-when-draw-instanced-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109468</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a bug where the 'RectTransform' component values were set to NaN when scaling UI elements using the Scene Rect Tool. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/rect-transform-values-are-set-to-nan-when-scaling-ui-element-with-centered-scaling" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102690</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a bug where the Cameras overlay would only open one Property Editor at a time, and if you changed the camera selection it would not open a new Property Editor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/cameras-overlay-does-not-open-the-correct-camera-component-properties-window-when-a-different-cameras-component-properties-window-was-opened" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115167</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a bug where the grids & snapping overlay as a toolbar is slightly higher because of the float field padding.

-   Editor: Fixed a bug where the Main Toolbar Overlay window was showing in preferences. (STO-3647)

-   Editor: Fixed a bug where when the user switches themes after reverting the background color preference, the light theme default override the dark theme default and vice versa. (STO-3645)

-   Editor: Fixed a bug whereby when evaluating an interrupted transition, the Animator writes the last evaluated values for all values instead of correctly defaulting to the state's current configuration. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/all-animators-previous-animations-are-activated-while-transitioning-when-a-second-split-off-transition-overrides-the-first-split-off-transition" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99493</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a crash in the Editor that occurred when calling `AssetBundle.UnloadAsync(...)` followed immediately by `AssetBundle.LoadFromFileAsync(...)` for the same AssetBundle. (UUM-108651)

-   Editor: Fixed a crash in the Model Importer when a FBX file has corrupted Blendshape data. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-unityfbx-convertfbxscenetoimportscene-when-importing-a-specific-fbx-file" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109143</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a crash on macOS that could occur in specific situations when entering Play Mode. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-nsapplication-endmodalsession-when-entering-play-mode-in-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109764</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a crash that occurred when an already opened project was opened by a second instance of the Editor in batch mode. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-zwwaitformultipleobjects-when-opening-the-same-project-more-than-once" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114810</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a crash that occurred when searching for certain attributes in user C# code. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-mono-custom-attrs-has-attr-when-opening-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115148</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a crash that rarely occurred on old IOS devices when the total attachment size exceeds the pixel storage limit. (UUM-100073)

-   Editor: Fixed a crash that would occur when some engine textures are used during shadow pass. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-getreflectionsample-when-using-shadergraph-with-dither-and-alpha-clip" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115024</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a crash when an internal source generated method is missing. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-scripting-method-invoke-when-the-player-is-build-before-entering-the-play-mode-in-the-editor" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108338</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a layout issue in the UI Toolkit Debugger toolbar where text overlapped when resizing the window. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-toolkit-bug-bundle-may-1-2025" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104492</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a null exception for docked Search window after domain reload. (UUM-112082)

-   Editor: Fixed a null reference exception in the Layer Inspector when removing items. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/deleting-multiple-tags-throws-nullreferenceexception-and-retrieving-array-element-that-was-out-of-bounds-errors-when-holding-the-enter-key" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119530</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a race condition in the licensing client where the Editor would succeed to make the client acquire a floating license faster than it had finished loading licenses at startup, which would make it fail to pick up the new license. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/file-watcher-does-not-trigger-after-the-acquirefloatinglease-and-before-the-entitlement-check" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121408</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a slight performance regression in build pipeline. (UUM-108628)

-   Editor: Fixed a thread issue when using matchMaterialPreset. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/text-render-issues-and-various-exceptions-in-textcore-when-using-textsettings-option-match-material-presets" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-124907</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a unwanted/needed folder. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-learning-templates-contains-an-empty-folder-the-hdrp-vfx-learning-templates-scene" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114923</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed a warning about debug symbols not being enabled for Cloud Diagnostics that could appear when the Cloud Diagnostics package wasn't installed. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-debug-symbols-shows-a-warning-that-cloud-diagnostics-is-enabled-when-cloud-diagnostics-package-is-not-installed" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109016</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Acceleration Structure Build Flags checkbox not working in Renderer inspector when lightmap preview foldout is open. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/option-for-acceleration-structure-build-flags-cannot-be-toggled-when-lightmaps-have-been-generated-for-a-mesh-renderer" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116585</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed alignment issues in PresetManager UI. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/broken-ui-in-default-preset-add-section-of-preset-manager-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110983</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an bug that was causing error spam to the console if an HDRP scene had a terrain without GPU instancing enabled and wireframe mode was turned on in the Scene View. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unable-to-add-renderer-to-the-scene-after-culling-dot-error-when-adding-a-terrain-in-a-hdrp-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104823</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an Editor crash happening when .shader files contain raygeneration, miss or callable shaders. These shader types are supported in .raytrace files only. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/dx12-crash-on-shaderlab-subprogram-compile-or-freeze-when-compiling-a-specific-ray-tracing-shader" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109230</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an Editor crash that would occur when loading single byte asset meta files. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-unityyaml-read-parseplainscalar-when-opening-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-125809</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an editor crash which occurring in projects using split native graphics jobs. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-dynamicbranchstate-preparestate-when-idle-in-a-project-with-specific-shaders" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-126695</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an error that occurred while loading a resource file when Cloud Diagnostics reporting is enabled. (UUM-107505)

-   Editor: Fixed an exception when accessing the toolbar of the animator window with a deleted animator. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/animator-window-has-a-dropdown-button-that-throws-missingreferenceexception-error-on-a-new-project-when-the-previous-project-had-a-gameobject-with-an-animation" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107430</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue in Build Profiles where script defines were applied without validation. (UUM-84855)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue in the imgui's charField. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/public-char-variables-uneditable-in-inspector-when-project-setting-use-imgui-default-inspector-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122800</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue in the UI Toolkit Samples window where all parent tabs displayed the same Numeric Fields preview. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-toolkit-bug-bundle-may-1-2025" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104492</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue that could cause the Editor Launch Screen to spontaneously crash on Linux. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-crash-on-editorlaunchscreen-platformeditorlaunchscreen-setprogress-when-opening-a-project-and-progress-bar-is-showing" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104828</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where a Raycast did not hit when using the default `BoxcastCommand()` constructor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/no-valid-hits-are-returned-when-using-raycastcommand" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-123124</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where a Raycast did not hit when using the default `CapsulecastCommand()` constructor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/no-valid-hits-are-returned-when-using-raycastcommand" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-123124</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where a Raycast did not hit when using the default `RaycastCommand()` constructor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/no-valid-hits-are-returned-when-using-raycastcommand" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-123124</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where a Raycast did not hit when using the default `SpherecastCommand()` constructor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/no-valid-hits-are-returned-when-using-raycastcommand" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-123124</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where camera rotation could cause ShadowCaster2Ds to be incorrectly clipped. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/shadowcaster2d-breaks-on-certain-rotation-positions-when-casting-source-is-set-to-polygoncollider2d" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-90714</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where changes to fields that used delayed fields were not saved in prefab scenes. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/values-in-rect-transform-component-are-only-partially-updated-when-changing-values-in-prefab-mode" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107890</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where entering Playmode with the Save Preset window open would throw an error in the console. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unityexception-getinvalidfilenamechars-errors-are-thrown-and-the-overlay-save-preset-button-becomes-unusable-when-entering-play-mode-with-a-shortcut-while-overlay-save-preset-window-is-opened" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119359</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where fatal error messages from import worker processes caused the main Editor process to terminate. Fatal error messages from workers are now reported by the main Editor process with a `(fatal flag from worker ignored)` suffix. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-ondemandscheduler-handleimportworkerlogs-when-performing-various-unity-operations" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115872</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where horizontal scrolling was not functional in the UI Toolkit Samples window snippets. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-toolkit-bug-bundle-may-1-2025" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104492</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where most Project Settings panels did not repaint automatically after entering Play Mode with *Reload Scene only* or *Do not reload Domain or Scene* settings enabled. The panels now refresh correctly without requiring re-selection of the category. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/project-settings-panels-disappear-and-do-not-repaint-when-entering-play-mode-without-reloading-domain" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108340</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where popup menus with submenus could be placed incorrectly on macOS when deep submenu items were selected. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/macos-rendering-debugger-volumes-component-dropdown-is-opened-with-a-big-offset-when-component-field-is-set-to-any-sample-scene" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116574</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where progress messages displayed during editor startup on Windows would sometimes stop updating. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/launch-screen-progress-bar-is-stuck-on-initialize-asset-database-when-opening-a-project-with-different-editor-version" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110166</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where renaming build profiles with capitalization changes incorrectly added a suffix '1' to the profile name. Profile names now update correctly without adding a suffix when capitalization is the only change. (UUM-104025)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where Scene view gizmo icons were not loaded properly if "Load textures on demand" was enabled in project settings. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/load-texture-data-on-demand-corrupts-unitys-packages-gizmos-in-scene-view" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104825</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where sky contribution was missing in fog when sky occlusion was enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/volumetric-fog-is-not-rendered-around-gameobjects-that-are-baked-with-apv-when-sky-occlusion-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119276</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where specular highlights appeared in the terrain grass shader. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/a-glare-from-the-light-placement-tool-is-visible-when-the-main-camera-is-set-to-perspective" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113119</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the "Load texture data on demand" editor option was erroneously being applied to textures that have their data stored in a temporary memory location. (These textures must read their texture data immediately). (UUM-77958)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the *More options* menu for IRenderPipelineGraphicsSettings in *Project Settings* > *Graphics Settings* wasn't visible when using the light theme. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urp-tab-reset-menu-button-is-invisible-in-light-mode" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112618</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the bottom of the horizontal scrollbar would be cut off when the Frame Debugger was maximized. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/frame-debugger-scrollbar-gets-cut-off-at-the-bottom-when-the-frame-debugger-window-is-maximised" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112097</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the CubemapArray inspector would not render correctly (while spamming errors in the console) under certain circumstances. (UUM-100574)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the Editor launch screen for Windows closed when pressing *Enter*. It now only closes when pressing the *Esc* key. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editor-launch-screen-will-close-when-enter-is-pressed-on-it" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111095</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the Frame value field didn't update when using the increment/decrement arrows in the Frame Debugger if the value was manually inputted. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-frame-value-field-does-not-update-when-using-the-increment-slash-decrement-arrows-in-the-frame-debugger-if-the-value-has-been-manually-inputted" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107394</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the ModelImporter AnimationClip transform mask user interface was not applied. (UUM-115035)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the preview popup would not be displayed when importing a custom package and clicking on a preview able asset. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/asset-preview-is-not-generated-when-selecting-an-asset-with-the-mouse-in-the-import-unity-package-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113219</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the search bar overlapped with other buttons in the Import Activity window when the window size was too small. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/import-activity-window-search-bar-overlaps-with-other-toolbar-buttons-when-docked" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110768</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the touchscreen could become stuck if lots of touch events were sent while the main thread is busy. (UUM-110835)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where the Unity logo in the main toolbar would shrink when the toolbar width was reduced. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unity-logo-becomes-deformed-when-the-editor-is-resized-horizontally" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110070</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where Unity Editor icons in the Inspector were not displayed correctly on high-DPI monitors (UUM-104627)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where using EditorUtility.CopySerialized with textures could break the Texture Streaming's texture size tracking. (UUM-77958)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where using the "Select Texture" window would, in certain specific scenarios, spam the console with errors related to an uninitialized variable. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/2d-urp-error-spam-when-textures-in-select-texture-window-dont-have-description" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119204</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue where VFX instances were not repainted immediately when modifying certain asset properties. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-are-not-reinit-immediatly-when-changing-properties-in-asset-inspector" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99917</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed an issue which could cause moving the Editor Launch Screen to appear jittery on Windows on scaled displays. (UUM-103498)

-   Editor: Fixed an out-of-range error that occurred when deleting text with right-to-left (RTL) languages. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/argumentoutofrangeexception-error-is-thrown-when-trying-to-delete-letters-from-an-rtl-textfield-when-using-the-advanced-text-generator" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116909</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed anchored overlays sometimes creating deadzones in the scene view. (UUM-105555)

-   Editor: Fixed applying SerializeReference root property overrides from prefab instances to prefab assets when the managed object already exists in the asset. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/serialized-unityevent-data-is-lost-when-array-elements-are-reordered-and-applied-to-prefab-via-modified-component-menu" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122778</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed artifacts in console window when using Log Entry = 1 with an empty string. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/debug-dot-log-empty-string-displays-visual-artifacts-in-console-when-log-entry-in-console-is-set-to-1-line" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112357</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed artifacts when using a Google Pixel device under Android 14-15, the Video Player, and when changing the resolution dynamically. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/display-issues-in-videoplayer-when-using-screen-dot-setresolution-on-certain-google-pixel-devices" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108144</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed artifacts when using HDRP with Screen Space Reflections in XR. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/quest-artifacts-when-using-hdrp-with-screen-space-reflections-in-xr" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-84213</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Awaitables that use main thread affinity logic for continuations so they are not hanging when paused in play mode. (UUM-110950)

-   Editor: Fixed Bind Texture script link in Water sample Cave scene. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/missing-script-error-when-clicking-script-link-in-cave-scenes-water-sample-description" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104561</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed BRG raw buffer binding when no metadata. (UUM-110313)

-   Editor: Fixed bug where Overlay dragger highlights even when it not intractable. (STO-3563)

-   Editor: Fixed carriage return in InputField on Windows. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/textfield-lines-overlap-when-editing-a-pasted-text" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-76174</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Color Field - Enabling seeing the EyeDropper color when inspecting Mixed values. (UUM-110400)

-   Editor: Fixed Color Field - Switching apps broke the color picker while doing the eye dropper. Esc applied the last hovered color. (UUM-110399)

-   Editor: Fixed ColorPicker incorrectly assigning the alpha to the blue channel. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/incorrect-color-is-selected-when-selecting-color-from-swatches-or-defaults-in-the-color-picker" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110106</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Console selection when logs are cleared, collapsed or filtered. (UUM-114787)

-   Editor: Fixed corrupted kernings. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/font-kerning-breaks-at-runtime-when-multiple-ui-documents-use-different-fonts" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121371</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed corruption issue when opening the about box a second time after moving the main window to another monitor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/windows-about-unity-window-needs-to-be-opened-twice-to-adapt-to-resolution" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-92333</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed crash on RenderTarget::SetActive when opening the project. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-rendertarget-setactive-when-opening-the-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103185</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed crash that could occur when repeatedly performing actions that require a progress dialog such as saving and entering play mode at the same time. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-nsapplication-endmodalsession-when-saving-while-play-mode-is-loading" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104261</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed crash when inputting long text in IMGUI. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-material-getshader-when-pasting-an-unusually-large-block-of-text-into-a-gameobjects-name-field" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120863</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed CreateInspectorGUI being called twice when Inspector was hidden for custom editors. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-editor-dot-createinspectorgui-method-is-being-called-twice-when-the-console-and-the-inspector-windows-are-in-the-same-panel" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-74710</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed cursor offset for IMGUI. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/caret-is-not-being-offset-when-content-on-a-guiskin-is-offset" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113827</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Delayed Touchscreen field with placeholder to register callback properly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-registervaluechangedcallback-is-not-called-when-isdelayed-is-set-to-true" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-77490</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed depth priming being disabled in reflection probes. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/depth-priming-is-ignored-when-rendering-a-reflection-probe-in-urp" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99152</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed disappearing caret for TMP InputField. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/tmps-input-field-caret-disappears-when-its-width-is-set-to-1" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104598</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed distorted TagManagerInspector pop up window if tag confirmed using Enter. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-tag-popup-window-is-deformed-and-stretched-out-when-creating-a-tag-with-the-same-name-as-an-already-existing-tag" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110586</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Dynamic panels drop zones being hardly usable. (STO-3640)

-   Editor: Fixed Edit > Delete option for gradient editor keys. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/cant-delete-gradient-keys-with-mac-keyboard-or-context-menu-options" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122403</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed editor crash when rotating in device simulator. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-editor-crashes-on-ui-canvas-updatecanvasrecttransform-when-rotating-2-times-in-the-device-simulator" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111731</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed EditorToolbarDropdown wrongly showing a text element in specific scenario. (UUM-107916)

-   Editor: Fixed EditorToolbarDropdownToggle hides label when in vertical layout but shows in horizontal. (UUM-105065)

-   Editor: Fixed failed assert when running Code Coverage builds (UUM-122729)

-   Editor: Fixed failed assertions in Rect::Clamp when trying to draw SelectionGrid. (UUM-109225)

-   Editor: Fixed false warning that displayed *BatchRendererGroups currently don't support override shaders*. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/warning-batchrenderergroups-currently-dont-support-override-shaders-is-spammed-in-play-mode-when-using-h-trace-wsgi-with-batchrendergroups" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122367</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed fied object reflection missing on a Planar Reflection Probe when rotating the camera to certain angles with the Realtime Raytracing quality setting enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/object-reflection-missing-on-a-planar-reflection-probe-when-rotating-the-camera-to-certain-angles-with-the-realtime-raytracing-quality-setting-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-86524</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed floating license cleanup and concurrent license update in LicensingClient 1.17.1.

-   Editor: Fixed for 'MemoryStream is corrupted' fatal error, when a modified prefab with prefab instance has its mono script deleted while at the same time a compilation error also is introduced. (UUM-82391)

-   Editor: Fixed for loadable plugin directories being marked as directories when root folder is reimported. Fix updates source db version. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/errors-are-logged-when-importing-an-asset-at-a-path-with-firebase" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115935</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed for missing "Some values driven by GridLayoutGroup" when users alter elements under parent/sibling LayoutGroup. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/some-values-driven-by-gridlayoutgroup-message-disappears-on-a-child-ui-gameobject-when-disabling-a-non-parent-gameobject" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-100091</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Game View performance drop in Editor Player Settings. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/game-view-performance-drops-significantly-when-player-settings-are-opened-in-project-settings" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120344</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed GetMainAssetTypeAtPath/GetMainAssetFromGUID returned the wrong script type when a name conflict was present (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/assetdatabase-dot-getmainassettypeatpath-returns-the-incorrect-type-when-using-a-global-namespace-class-with-the-same-name-as-a-unity-type" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-86895</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed global scene list not updating while custom build profile is active. (UUM-104738)

-   Editor: Fixed hang where the Editor could hang on Linux when running under a misconfigured display session. (UUM-101798)

-   Editor: Fixed HDRP random crash when changing quality settings. (UUM-96844)

-   Editor: Fixed HierarchyV2 to now support FindInScene functionality. (RAT-2289)

-   Editor: Fixed IME issue when focusing out of a UITK field. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/selected-korean-input-text-in-uitoolkit-textfield-is-not-fully-replaced-when-refocusing-and-entering-new-input" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122283</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed IME on Blur() for UIToolkit. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/textfield-in-uitoolkit-is-not-cleared-when-entering-korean-characters" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102003</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed IMGUI EditorGUIUtility.labelWidth value being changed by the Scene View UI. This could impact other windows drawn afterwards. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editorguilayout-dot-toggle-clickable-area-is-shifted-when-using-built-in-render-pipeline" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110450</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed IMGUI Errors on LinuxEditor arising when RepaintImmediately() called on window currently being painted. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-using-ctrl-plus-alt-plus-a-shortcut-for-add-component-throws-gui-rendering-errors" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104622</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Inconsistent color scheme in "Details" section of "Select Presets" inspector window. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/inconsistent-color-scheme-in-details-section-of-select-presets-inspector-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111270</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed inconsistent naming in the UI Toolkit Samples window. All items are now displayed in a consistent format using human-readable names. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-toolkit-bug-bundle-may-1-2025" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104492</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed incorrect margins for animator state inspector window. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/script-component-header-has-a-space-in-the-inspector-window-when-adding-script-for-state-machine" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111381</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed incorrect margins in state machine inspector. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/script-component-header-has-a-space-in-the-inspector-window-when-adding-script-for-state-machine" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111381</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed incorrect margins in the UI Toolkit Project Settings pane. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-toolkit-bug-bundle-may-1-2025" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104492</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed InspectorTitlebar always showing enabled state when no enabled SerializedProperty was provided. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editorguilayout-dot-inspectortitlebar-appears-enabled-when-inspecting-a-disabled-monobehaviour-component" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112560</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue of Unlit draw mode in the scene view of an HDRP scene. (Object is not rendered in DX12 and Vulkan if the editor is launched with Unlit draw mode). (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/terrain-is-not-rendered-and-a-warning-is-thrown-when-unlit-draw-mode-is-used-in-the-scene-view" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110322</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue on macOS where a dropdown can appear very far away from the button that was clicked. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builder-dropdown-options-appear-on-top-of-the-window-when-part-of-the-window-is-covered" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109011</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue on macOS with progress such as script reloading taking focus from other applications when the editor was in the background. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/focus-changes-from-the-ide-to-the-unity-editor-when-compiling-code-in-the-ide" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113123</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue when entering play mode can cause a crash on macOS when under heavy load. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-nsapplication-endmodalsession-when-entering-play-mode-in-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109764</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue where an incorrect error message was displayed when attempting to load an overlay preset that did not match target window type. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unclear-error-pop-up-appears-when-loading-incorrect-preset-from-disk" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115655</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue where components were visible in the Add Component menu when \[AddComponentMenu("")\] was used. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/component-is-not-hidden-from-add-component-menu-when-using-addcomponentmenu" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99267</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue where dependency shader changes were not properly detected in Terrain splat materials which resulting in the usage of Unity's error shader for rendering that geometry. (UUM-117516)

-   Editor: Fixed issue where excluded Quality Settings were being included in builds on some platforms. (UUM-108631)

-   Editor: Fixed issue where the CrashReporter was improperly getting EngineDiagnostics value during runtime.

-   Editor: Fixed issue where the Unity Editor would still open after a user terminated the launch screen during project load. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-editor-opens-when-the-window-is-closed-right-after-launch" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107863</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue with a Build Profile tooltip not showing correct path to Build Profile asset if it was moved.

-   Editor: Fixed issue with CheckerboardBackground so can now zoom maximum percentage in UIBuilder and will not exceed vertex budget. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builder-viewport-is-rendered-in-solid-black-and-argumentoutofrangeexception-is-thrown-when-zooming-in" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105805</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue with static time access to BuildTargetDiscovery causes importer to fail. (UUM-105132)

-   Editor: Fixed issue with TMP bloom effects not working anymore. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/non-hdr-color-picker-opens-when-selecting-material-color-with-hdr-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-90091</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed issue with wording in the Engine Diagnostics section of the Build Profile window. (UUM-110263)

-   Editor: Fixed layout issue causing an unnecessary horizontal scrollbar to appear in Preset Inspector. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/an-extra-not-needed-scrollbar-becomes-visible-in-the-preset-inspector-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111255</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed LinuxEditor failing to trigger some shortcuts when InputManager is disabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-shortcuts-that-use-shift-modifier-and-numbers-row-are-not-triggered" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104604</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Manual license activation flow via command-line. (UUM-122146)

-   Editor: Fixed measure and word-wrapping instabilities with Text. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/label-text-overflows-its-rect-when-resizing-the-game-view-in-free-aspect" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-79773</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed method decorated with HideInCallstack attribute being opened by IDE instead of the caller method. -   Editor: Fixed minor UI overlapping text in the HDRP Wizard. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdrp-wizard-window-ui-components-overlap-when-hdrp-wizard-window-is-docked-and-resized-horizontally" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105020</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed missing help url in the property inspector (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-parameters-are-missing-the-documentation-links" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114092</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed missing icons for brush attributes and target density for built-in Terrain Tools.

-   Editor: Fixed New EntityId not displaying on the Inspector in Debug mode. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/instanceid-is-missing-from-the-debug-inspector" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112982</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed null exception in saved search. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-error-is-thrown-when-gameobject-preview-is-converted-to-a-floating-window-from-a-saved-search-in-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112608</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed null exception on thread with ATG and null FontAsset. (UUM-121615)

-   Editor: Fixed null exception on threads with TextCore. (UUM-117136)

-   Editor: Fixed null material pointer crash in IMGUI. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-pptr-operator-shader-star-const-when-calling-resources-dot-unloadunusedassets-in-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117563</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed OutOfRange error encountered while using the 'Edit the joint angular limits' button in the Articulation Body component. -   Editor: Fixed OverlayMenu behaviour when deleting or reverting presets. -   Editor: Fixed packages selected will be kept even when switched away to other platforms on platform discovery window when creating build profiles. (UUM-104814)

-   Editor: Fixed perceptual smoothness in Specular occlusion for shader graphs. (UUM-122489)

-   Editor: Fixed possible crashes and exceptions when triggering a scene save from script with an unsaved "Untitled" scene. -   Editor: Fixed preview drag handler not being correctly hidden when preview is shown as floating window. -   Editor: Fixed previous splash screen background/logo from being cleared. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/splash-image-logo-is-shown-after-being-removed-when-pressing-the-preview-button" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111658</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed problem with inspector UI throwing exceptions when both script and UI document assets are moved. -   Editor: Fixed project browser to now show correct icons when item is selected. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/prefab-icon-blends-in-when-selected-in-project-window-with-the-light-editor-theme-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107996</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed proper ordering of buttons in the footer of the build profile window. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/buttons-cloud-build-and-switch-profile-changed-their-positions" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105431</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed regression that caused the wrong info / help icons to show in the editor UI for the SRPs. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/wrong-documentation-icon-in-multiple-graphics-tools" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104996</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed rendering debugger warning when disabling a setting in the HDRP asset. (UUM-110804)

-   Editor: Fixed Screen Space Lens Flare override inspector error when there no render (game or scene view). (UUM-110866)

-   Editor: Fixed selection issue on Android when using GameActivity. (UUM-104765)

-   Editor: Fixed serialization issues with the Customizable toolbar.

-   Editor: Fixed Shader Graph sticky note from losing visibility of first line after Enter key pressed. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/sticky-note-body-text-disappears-when-pressing-the-enter-key" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-76191</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed slow impostor baking when Split Jobs threading mode is used. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-camera-dot-render-hangs-the-process-of-geometry-baking-in-the-textures-when-using-hdrp" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-75914</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed some Typos in the documentation. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/typos-in-vfx-learning-samples-spawn-context-and-orient-showcase-pages" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114891</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed t: that was inserting a pill (<a href="https://jira.unity3d.com/browse/RAT-2271\" class="underline hover:no-underline">https://jira.unity3d.com/browse/RAT-2271\</a>). (RAT-2271)

-   Editor: Fixed text clip issue with IMGUI (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/certain-lowercase-letters-are-cropped-in-the-inspector-when-using-system-font-mode" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-98223</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed the Accelerator client code from hanging when uploads are canceled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unity-editor-hangs-on-a-fresh-import-when-using-cacheserverwaitforuploadcompletion-command-line-argument" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113816</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed the bitmap system font to match the operating system's font smoothing setting. -   Editor: Fixed the issue with search results are stacked on top of each other. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/search-results-are-stacked-on-top-of-each-other" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107495</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed the link that opens when clicking on the ? icon in the RayTracingShader (.raytrace) inspector in the Editor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ray-tracing-shader-link-to-the-documentation-isnt-working" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122368</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed Water Decals Foam UV. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/water-decals-do-not-render-to-anchored-position-in-local-space-when-the-distant-wind-speed-is-set-to-a-higher-value" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111267</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed when a BuildProfile is renamed, update the BuildProfileEditor to refresh the name displayed. (UUM-104263)

-   Editor: Fixed wrong warning about best-fit usage in uss. (UUM-119250)

-   Editor: Fixed `EditorBuildSettings.scenes` when using `InitializeOnLoadMethod` during startup.

-   Editor: Fixed `ExposedReference` property drawer not allowing scene references. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/drag-and-drop-and-object-picker-assignment-fail-when-exposedreference-variables-are-used-in-a-timeline-clip" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108754</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed `GetIcons` API for Standalone platforms. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/player-settings-icons-are-not-retrieved-when-using-playersettings-dot-geticons-method" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-97474</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed `HeaderAttribute` property drawer issue when used with collections. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/headerattribute-does-not-work-when-used-with-arrays-or-lists" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104395</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed `PlayerSettings` icon APIs so that `GetIcons`, `GetIconsForTargetGroup`, `GetIconSizes`, and `GetIconSizesForTargetGroup` return the correct icon arrays for the provided `NamedBuildTarget` or `BuildTargetGroup` argument. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/player-settings-icons-are-not-retrieved-when-using-playersettings-dot-geticons-method" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-97474</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed: "Asset Labels" section should not appear when a non-asset is being selected. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/asset-labels-section-appears-in-inspector-when-selecting-a-scene-gameobject" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110356</a>)

-   Editor: Fixed: Ensure preference to accept to modify the Diagnostics page stays valid for the life of the SettingsWindow. -   Editor: Fixed: Ensure we do not rebuild the SearchBaseCollection view if the fixedItemHeight hasn't changed. -   Editor: Fixed: Exception thrown when renaming component with symbols in UIBuilder.     NullReferenceException thrown when Terrain component is removed and re-added with the existing Terrain data. (UUM-108146)

-   Editor: Place scroller in description of New Scene dialog (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/new-scene-window-ui-breaks-when-minimizing" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111789</a>)

-   Editor: Removing AssetBundle Patching API.

-   Editor: Report Editor architecture information

-   Editor: Unity_RendererBounds_Min/max available in URP with BRG, EG or GRD. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/different-custom-shader-behavior-when-gpu-resident-drawer-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103990</a>)

-   Editor: Updated 7-Zip to 25.00.

-   Editor: URP Decal Projector is not set dirty when creating a new Decal material. (UUM-112667)

-   Editor: \[MacOS\] Fixed a BeginLayoutGroup error on macOS that occurred when holding the Enter key if the tag already existed. -   EmbeddedLinux: Fixed main thread name setting. (UUM-116884)

-   Engine Diagnostics: Fixed a crash on UnityEngine::Insights::Metric::Metric caused by a race condition. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-unityengine-insights-metric-metric-when-refocusing-on-the-editor-after-it-has-been-idling-in-the-background" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113363</a>)

-   Engine Diagnostics: Fixed crash occurring when sending diagnostics data in the background. (UUM-110101)

-   Engine Diagnostics: Fixed Segmentation fault occurring when using --reset-diagnostic-switches via CLI on Linux. (UUM-111566)

-   Engine Diagnostics: Fixed user metadata not appearing for native crash reports on Diagnostics dashboard (UUM-121591)

-   GI: Avoided error code 5 during bake of a specific scene (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/error-light-baking-failed-with-error-code-5-convergence-data-not-available-while-rendering-lightmaps-thrown-in-console-when-generating-lighting-for-specific-gameobjects" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-98405</a>)

-   GI: Doing a Repaint() when we need to fetch a new texture. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/texture-map-viewer-shows-loading-dot-dot-dot-text-instead-of-indicating-users-that-baked-texture-maps-are-missing" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116168</a>)

-   GI: Enabled CPU lightmapper on Apple Silicon Editor and Windows on ARM Editor by running CPU lightmapper using x64 emulation in a separate process. (UUM-102347)

-   GI: Ensured that it is possible to do several successful APV bakes consecutively from script. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/incorrectly-baking-lighting-when-consecutively-loading-multiple-scenes-with-individual-baking-sets" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111364</a>)

-   GI: Ensured that the "RenderSettings customReflection texture has invalid type" error is show exactly when relevant. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/rendersettings-customreflection-texture-has-invalid-type-error-keeps-getting-thrown-even-when-environment-reflections-were-set-back-to-skybox-from-custom-with-an-invalid-texture" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121679</a>)

-   GI: Ensured that the APV "Unload These Scenes" button does not attempt to unload an unsaved scene. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/loading-unloaded-unsaved-scene-throws-argumentexception-error-in-the-console" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116738</a>)

-   GI: Ensured that the GI cache is cleaned-up according to the max size limit that is given by the user. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/gi-cache-exceeds-limit-when-using-batchmode-command-line-argument" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113152</a>)

-   GI: Ensured that we don't crash the Editor in a specific edge case, when a reflection probe is added to a scene which has never been saved and the scene is then baked without first explicitly saving the scene. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urp-crash-on-gameobject-querycomponentbytype-when-baking-a-reflection-probe-in-an-unsaved-slash-untitled-scene" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120770</a>)

-   GI: Fixed a bug where "Don't recalculate" probe placement mode sometimes doesn't work when GPU streaming is enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-occurs-when-baking-apv-with-gpu-streaming-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103688</a>)

-   GI: Fixed a bug where "per-vertex" SH evaluation mode didn't work with Deferred rendering URP. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/sh-evaluation-mode-has-no-performance-difference-when-set-to-per-vertex-compared-to-per-pixel" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104066</a>)

-   GI: Fixed a bug where projects using Adaptive Probe Volumes would have high memory usage in Editor, especially when the project contains many scenes that use Adaptive Probe Volumes. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/all-baked-data-for-all-scenes-using-apv-is-always-loaded-in-editor" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104833</a>)

-   GI: Fixed a bug where some column labels were incorrect in the Light explorer when using URP. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/columns-are-mislabeled-in-the-reflection-probes-section-of-light-explorer" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-83864</a>)

-   GI: Fixed a bug where the Override Rendering Layer Mask UI of the Probe Adjustment Volume component would throw errors when no Rendering Layer Masks are defined in the project. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/index-out-of-range-exception-when-trying-to-use-rendering-layer-override-in-probe-adjustment-volume-without-a-mask-defined-in-lighting-settings" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-95377</a>)

-   GI: Fixed a crash that occurred when no valid lightmap parameters were assigned. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-inputextraction-extractootssnapshot-when-opening-scene" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111154</a>)

-   GI: Fixed a TDR

-   GI: Fixed an issue where APV scenario blending is non-functional on MacOS. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/adaptive-probe-lighting-scenarios-are-immutable-when-in-play-mode" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-126723</a>)

-   GI: Fixed an issue where baking a scene containing a static Skinned Mesh Renderer and Adaptive Probe Volumes would throw an exception. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/invalidcastexception-specified-cast-is-not-valid-is-thrown-when-generating-lighting-with-virtual-offset-enabled-in-apv-and-a-static-skinned-mesh-rendered-with-a-mesh-filter-component-is-in-the-scene" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104711</a>)

-   GI: Fixed an issue where scenes lit entirely by adaptive probe volumes would have shadowmask and subtractive related keywords stripped out, even if they were required. Affected projects must do a clean build for the fix to take effect. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-baked-shadows-do-not-appear-in-the-player-when-distance-shadowmask-and-adoptive-probe-volume-are-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-84218</a>)

-   GI: Fixed an issue where using "Live Updates" probe placement mode in the rendering debugger would cause a progress bar to be shown on screen permanently. (UUM-110221)

-   GI: Fixed an issue where using a rotated 6-pass skybox shader would cause the lightmapper to produce NaN values. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/non-finite-value-in-temp-slash-lightbakeroutput-slash-lightmaprequest0-slash-star-dot-r2d-dot-errors-are-thrown-when-baking-a-lightmap" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-96043</a>)

-   GI: Fixed APV Virtual Offset baking on AMD. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/look-rotation-viewing-vector-is-zero-messages-are-logged-in-the-console-when-baking-lighting-with-display-probes-setting-enabled-in-rendering-debugger-while-using-the-android-build-profile-on-amd-hardware" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114563</a>)

-   GI: Fixed case where custom properties on a Renderer are not taken into account, leading to incorrectly baked lightmaps. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/material-property-block-emits-and-reflects-incorrect-color-when-baked-indirect-light-is-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108565</a>)

-   GI: Fixed issue so now APV work with Subscenes in URP. (UUM-104165)

-   GI: Fixed shader compiler warnings that occurred when opening a new project.

-   GI: Fixed using a style which respects themes for text. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/text-in-baked-shadowmask-mode-is-difficult-to-read-in-baked-lightmap-viewer-when-editor-theme-is-set-to-light" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116137</a>)

-   GI: Fixed using a wrapping style for text. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/informational-text-does-not-wrap-and-does-not-fit-when-the-viewer-window-is-opened-by-default" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116119</a>)

-   GI: Fixed: Added error handling and checks to ensure that the `JobProcess` is not attempted to be used on MacOS Arm64 unless Rosetta 2 is available.

-   GI: Fixed: Group lighting related settings in the UniversalRenderPipelineAsset.

-   GI: Fixed: Out of bounds mesh LOD assert during light baking that happened when a mesh had additional vertex streams, a non-zero GI mesh LOD index and additional vertex stream mesh without the same number of mesh LODs as the main mesh. (UUM-105370)

-   GI: Fixed: The size of Disc light was incorrectly calculated in HDRP path-tracing. (UUM-108082)

-   GI: Make baking of duplicated Probuilder meshes work. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/baked-global-illumination-produces-incorrect-lighting-colors-when-vertex-color-is-applied-on-a-duplicated-probuilder-gameobject" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-126739</a>)

-   GI: Raised the limit of probes that APV can handle and also introduced a descriptive error message for the console in case this new limit is exceeded (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/outofmemory-exception-in-the-console-and-unreleased-memory-when-baking-adaptive-probe-volumes" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103389</a>)

-   GI: Re-import cubemaps with specular convolution instead of re-baking reflection probes when switching platforms. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/reflection-problem-custom-cubemap-loses-its-reference-when-hdr-is-enabled-and-the-platform-is-switched" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111929</a>)

-   GI: Removed (dead) toolbar support code from HDRP.

-   GI: Rewrote parts of APV probe placement in order to 1) fix MacOS probe placement bug, and 2) make Windows and MacOS use the same code path. (UUM-73280)

-   GI: Subscenes now open before the APV baking starts if "Generate Lighting" is pressed in the lighting window. (UUM-104411)

-   GI: Updated the APV Enlighten warning message to be more descriptive (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/usability-warning-message-in-an-adaptive-probe-volume-component-is-not-informative-enough-when-realtime-global-illumination-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111952</a>)

-   GI: Valid mesh fails extraction for LightBaking. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/pathtracer-addgeometry-job-error-is-thrown-when-baking-lighting" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-101294</a>)

-   Graphics: Added job lifetime warnings when GraphicsStateCollection warmup spans many frames. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/warnings-when-warming-up-shaders-with-graphicsstatecollection" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102639</a>)

-   Graphics: Added missing initialization in EvaluateAdaptiveProbeVolume. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/shader-error-in-shader-graphs-slash-sg-unlit-warning-appears-and-material-fails-to-render-when-accessing-adaptive-probe-volume-data-through-evaluateadaptiveprobevolume-method" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110695</a>)

-   Graphics: Applied workaround for video black screen issue on Mali GPUs with OpenGLES. (UUM-116491)

-   Graphics: Avoid heap allocations when drawing APV gizmos. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/more-calls-are-made-to-gc-dot-alloc-in-an-hdrp-sample-template-when-compared-to-the-2022-dot-3-stream" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121201</a>)

-   Graphics: Clamp the override level for Mesh LOD to the mesh's LOD count in GRD's Mesh LOD selection to prevent out-of-range values. (UUM-112018)

-   Graphics: Disabled `CAMetalDisplayLink` on iOS to prevent potential GPU hangs and rendering freezes. It will be re-enabled once the underlying cause is identified and resolved. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/metal-game-freezes-after-command-buffer-timeout-error" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-123207</a>)

-   Graphics: Ensures corresponding VkFramebuffer(s) are destroyed when an FDM VkImage is destroyed. (UUM-113806)

-   Graphics: Fix editor freeze if Lens SRP asset references form a cyclic dependency (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-editor-freezes-when-creating-a-new-lens-flare-srp-and-adding-itself-to-the-lens-flare-data-srp-type-element" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122934</a>)

-   Graphics: Fix Vulkan freezing on primary display with Run in Background enabled (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/built-player-with-vulkan-graphics-api-hangs-slash-freezes-when-vsync-is-enabled-and-run-in-background-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113045</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed 'Shader Preloaded' being unmodifiable in Projectsettings > Graphics. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/value-of-the-size-field-in-the-preload-shaders-property-is-reverted-when-attempting-to-change-the-value" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111435</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed a bug where Mesh.GetIndexStart and Mesh.GetIndexCount always returned values for sub-mesh 0 in meshes with multiple LODs. (UUM-111791)

-   Graphics: Fixed a CPU crash happening when logging a DRED report generated after GPU crash when using -force-d3d12-debug argument. (UUM-115025)

-   Graphics: Fixed a crash or a blank screen being drawn when MSAA resolve to back buffer is requested on platforms that don't have support for it. Add error message to explain the issue. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-vulkan-msaa-gets-resolved-into-a-wrong-swap-chain-buffer-when-using-cameratarget-as-resolvetarget" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-60474</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed a crash that occurred when switching between scenes on Android when using the Vulkan graphics API. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-crash-on-atomicqueue-enqueue-atomicnode-star-plus-12-when-a-scene-transition-happens-in-the-player" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-82923</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed a crash when calling Mesh.GetIndices with a list and passing -1 to ignore mesh LODs. (UUM-111792)

-   Graphics: Fixed a potential deadlock that could freeze players when loading or unloading AssetBundles with threaded texture uploads enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/arm-the-player-freezes-when-awaiting-g-uploadedtexturecondvar-in-the-main-thread" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102896</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed a validation warning on DirectX by changing the placeholder input layout format. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/a-vertex-stream-with-a-stride-of-1-is-bound-to-the-gpu-in-player-when-mesh-is-compressed" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105001</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed additional separator in menu when the VolumeComponent is in the default VolumeProfile. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/double-separation-lines-are-present-in-override-controls-in-a-defaultvolumeprofile-asset" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108517</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed additional separator in menu when there is no advanced properties in the VolumeComponent. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/double-separation-lines-are-present-in-override-controls-in-a-new-volume-profile-asset" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108543</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed an error on certain platforms caused by an incorrect type of rendering layer in the shader for terrain's depth-normal prepass. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/terrainlitdepthnormalspass-is-using-uint4-instead-of-uint-for-outrenderinglayers" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117463</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed an issue in URP that could cause a GPU hang when lit pixels were almost clipping the far plane. (UUM-112640)

-   Graphics: Fixed an issue where ETC1 assets loaded from old AssetBundles appeared washed out when originally imported as sRGB and the project was set to use Linear color space. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-the-textures-are-brighter-slash-washed-out-when-unloading-a-scene-packaged-in-an-older-stream-from-assetbundles-on-android" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113599</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed an issue where performing certain actions such as baking reflection probes could cause assertions to be hit. (UUM-103689)

-   Graphics: Fixed an issue with "Rendering Layers" in Decal Projector contains the 24th layer or above on URP, decal projection does not work properly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/decals-are-not-projected-when-rendering-layers-are-set-to-24th-layer-or-above-in-decal-projector" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105697</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed BatchRendererGroup's OnPerformCulling to function now correctly checks for job safety between the provided buffers and the JobHandle returned by the user. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/no-error-slash-warning-in-console-when-batchrenderergroup-reads-draw-commands-before-culling-jobs-are-complete" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-98559</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed cloud shadows popping when no shadows are rendered from directional light. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/shadows-disappear-when-looking-at-a-certain-angle-in-scene-view-and-using-cloud-shadows-with-volumetric-clouds" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107688</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed corner case with native render pass emulation workflow where render target was not set correctly before clearing depth buffer.

-   Graphics: Fixed crash after disconnecting an external display on Android. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-crash-or-freeze-when-disconnecting-an-external-monitor-from-an-android-device" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110456</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed crashes when reading Texture2D (sub)assets with a data payload larger than expected. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-persistentmanager-getserializedfileifobjectavailable-when-opening-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-101784</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed Cubemap mipmaps display differently between Editor and Player when using custom mipmaps. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/cubemap-mipmaps-display-differently-between-editor-and-player-when-using-custom-mipmaps" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104071</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed duplicated color resolve when mixing native render pass and set render target workflows. (UUM-103751)

-   Graphics: Fixed error when opening Inspector of the LookDev volume component after creating a new HDRP 3D project. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-thrown-in-console-when-changing-profile-field-to-defaultlookdevprofile-in-lighting-window-after-deleting-directional-light" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116107</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed flickering of gpu instanced particle system in linux when using OpenGL. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-gpu-instanced-particles-rendering-issue-when-using-opengl" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-100915</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed flickering of gpu instanced particle system when using OpenGL. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/particle-systems-that-use-meshes-flicker-in-player-when-gpu-instancing-is-enabled-on-older-adreno-gpus" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-98222</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed GameView not always resetting when VolumeProfile is Reset. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/volume-profile-effects-are-still-showing-and-active-in-game-view-when-the-volume-profile-is-reset" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111415</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed GBuffer index mismatch in URP. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/some-textures-do-not-render-when-deferred-renderpass-is-selected" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109709</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed incorrect rendering of objects when using GPU Occlusion Culling on scenes containing mesh renderers using meshes with no triangles. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/graphical-issues-are-caused-by-gpu-occlusion-culling-when-using-amplify-shaders" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115962</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed incorrect sorting order with combined Text Mesh Pro and MeshRenderer objects when using GPU Resident Drawer. Objects like these now bypass GPU Resident Drawer. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/layer-render-order-is-not-respected-inside-the-android-player-when-using-gpu-resident-drawer" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115123</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed IndexOutOfRangeException when inserting a new level of detail in LODGroup. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/lod-group-levels-partially-grey-out-and-an-indexoutofrangeexception-error-gets-thrown-in-the-console-when-inserting-a-new-lod-group-level" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114504</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed issues when OpenXR resize it's swapchain a lot. (UUM-109456)

-   Graphics: Fixed memory corruption caused by inconsistent reflection probe metadata.

-   Graphics: Fixed MeshFilter excessively logging SendMessage warnings when used in Prefab. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/sendmessage-warning-is-thrown-when-selecting-a-prefab" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114459</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed missing buffer bindings for debug shaders in HDRP rendering debugger, which caused a rendering issue with an error message. This occured in certain conditions (e.g. path tracing was enabled) with the following windows:  

    -   material debug view (Rendering Debugger > Material)  
    -   cluster debug view (Rendering Debugger > Lighting > Tile/Cluster Debug > Cluster). (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/rendering-debugger-throws-a-warning-and-renders-the-scene-view-in-solid-black-when-material-debug-tools-and-path-tracing-are-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103401</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed missing Light theme for Volume inspector icon. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/light-theme-icon-is-missing-for-volume-component-in-the-inspector-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111418</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed missing shader binding when PrecomputedAtmosphericAttenuation is set to zero in ShaderConfig.cs and ShaderConfig.cs.hlsl  
    This error was causing graphical issues such as broken ocean lighting, on top of error messages. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdrp-water-renders-black-when-the-precomputedatmosphericattenuation-is-set-to-zero" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117118</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed occasional crash on exit. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-playermain-int-char-const-star-star-when-exiting-standalone-player-with-a-particle-system-in-the-scene" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120761</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed occasional crash when changing shader in the inspector to the one with GrabPass. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-mono-dump-native-crash-info-when-changing-a-particle-system-renderers-material-shader-to-standard-unlit" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121727</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed perpetually increasing procedural indirect draw calls in HDRP's Volumetric Fog pass when connecting a player build to the Frame Debugger. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/frame-debuggers-hierarchy-is-not-navigable-when-connecting-it-to-the-project-built-with-the-volumetric-fog" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107818</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed potential race conditions in `CalculateSkinningMatrices` by ensuring direct job completion for improved safety. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-crash-on-skinnedmeshrenderermanager-calculateskinningmatrices" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111486</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed random GPU hang when using GPU Occlusion Culling on certain platforms. (UUM-112854)

-   Graphics: Fixed reflection probes not working correctly with GPUResidentDrawer. Force enable probe blending & atlas when it's in use. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/reflection-probe-is-not-rendered-when-changing-the-gpu-resident-drawer-or-probe-blending-setting" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109241</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed shadow culling not working correctly for intermediate renderers like Graphics.RenderMesh or Terrain. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/terrain-detail-mesh-triangles-value-increases-when-occlusion-culling-is-used-and-cast-shadows-is-enabled-on-the-mesh" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-96217</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed sporadic crash when using Cloth rendering with Vulkan. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-crash-on-gfxdevicevkbase-drawbuffersbatchmode-in-the-player-when-loading-slash-unloading-character-models" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-90932</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed the Color Picker HDR swatches so they now store the full color value instead of the `color.gamma` value. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/incorrect-color-values-are-saved-for-swatches-when-using-hdr-color-picker" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121056</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed truncation issues with MVPVV.

-   Graphics: Fixed undisposed array in GPUResidentDrawer.ProcessRendererMaterialAndMeshChanges. (UUM-108147)

-   Graphics: Fixed unreferenced assets not being destroyed when switching scenes or calling Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets when using GPU Resident Drawer. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/memory-usage-rises-when-switching-scenes-with-grd-enabled-and-a-loaded-texture-in-urp" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120539</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed Vulkan Validation error in vkQueueSubmit (UUM-110090)

-   Graphics: Fixed: Compressed texture atlases no longer produce invalid mipmaps for smaller atlas items by limiting the number of miplevels. This is based on the miplevel where the smallest atlas item is exactly one compression block large. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/texture2d-dot-packtextures-causes-mips-7-9-to-turn-pink-when-packed-textures-are-of-astc-4x4-compression" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-97688</a>)

-   Graphics: Fixed: The texture FilterMode would default to Point filtering on some iOS and OSX devices when mipmap streaming was enabled, this has been fixed. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/texture-looks-point-sampled-when-mipmap-streaming-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107360</a>)

-   Graphics: Improved the error message when trying to use Volume framework before initialization. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-is-thrown-when-trying-to-access-volumestack-from-the-hdcamera-class" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104542</a>)

-   Graphics: Increased the maximum amount of UAVs that can be bound to RayTracingShaders from 8 to 64. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/errors-there-are-more-uavs-9-than-the-maximum-supported-8-in-ray-tracing-shader-raytracinguavbug-dot-and-raytracinguavbug-dot-raytrace-ray-tracing-program-is-invalid-are-present-when-using-more-than-8-uavs" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112727</a>)

-   Graphics: Removed an assert exception in UnityEngine.Rendering.CreateDrawBatchesJob.ProcessRenderer when changing a material property of a material used in MeshRenderers with multiple submeshes, after re-importing URP or HDRP. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/assertionexception-thrown-and-mesh-turns-invisible-when-changing-material-offset-manually-via-the-inspector-or-through-settextureoffset-on-a-mesh-with-more-than-one-material" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103975</a>)

-   Graphics: Removed GC allocations caused by RayTracingAccelerationStructure.AddInstances. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/raytracingaccelerationstructure-dot-addinstances-causes-gc-allocations" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121305</a>)

-   Graphics: Removed GPUResidentDrawerResources.asset which has been obsolete since 2023.3.0a14. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-gpuresidentdrawerresources-script-file-is-missing-from-the-scriptable-render-pipeline-core-package" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120113</a>)

-   Graphics: Removed redundant target requirements from APV includes in URP. (UUM-97906)

-   Graphics: This PR fixes the issue with water fade. The displacement of the water was fading too much if the water mesh is far from the origin. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdrp-water-displacement-fades-when-camera-move-far-in-worldspace" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111903</a>)

-   Graphics: This PR fixes the light flickering issue when deferred, shadow, and TAA are enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/lights-are-flickering-when-using-deferred-or-deferred-plus-rendering-with-shadows-and-taa-enabled-in-urp" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113351</a>)

-   Graphics: Updated UX and documentation to reflect global behavior of Advanced Properties in SRP Inspectors. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/water-surface-components-appearance-section-advanced-properties-are-enabling-them" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102665</a>)

-   Graphics: URP: Fixed an issue where specific configurations would cause \_ProjectionParams.x to become incorrectly negated for some cameras. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/scene-view-has-y-coordinates-of-the-screen-position-node-flipped-when-some-of-the-urp-features-are-disabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109860</a>)

-   Graphics: When the "Load texture data on demand" setting was enabled, sometimes textures would not be properly loaded in the editor and result in empty or missing texture errors in the console. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/certain-textures-are-not-loaded-when-using-the-load-texture-data-on-demand-feature" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114482</a>)

-   Graphics: When UpdateExternalTexture is called it will Unregister the previous texture to free the memory. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/memory-increases-indefinitely-when-playing-vlc-video-stream" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105771</a>)

-   Graphics Tests: Fixed an issue where multiple "Player" graphics tests in a single scene failed to find references. (UUM-109000)
