-   Graphics Tests: Fixed corruption of Variable Rate Shading states in GfxDevice. (UUM-91468)

-   GraphView: Added Pointer events to handle edge connection and manipulation. Enables touch input to be properly addressed. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/graph-nodes-cant-be-connected-using-touch-screen-controls" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-73221</a>)

-   HDRP: Added a missing documentation link for the High Quality Line Renderer volume component in Graphics Settings. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdrp-graphics-high-quality-line-rendering-is-missing-a-documentation-link" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102165</a>)

-   HDRP: Cached shadows now removes a light if an undo operation affects the light. This is done to avoid having invalid references which would cause a crash. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-rendererscene-notifyinvisible-when-entering-play-mode-after-undoing-a-change-within-the-scene-with-ctrl-plus-z" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116010</a>)

-   HDRP: Color Checker - Fixed overlap of the label when the inspector width is narrow. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/color-checker-is-metallic-text-overlaps-color-field-when-inspector-window-is-minimised" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113853</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed All button is not presented using the macOS system. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdrp-wizard-fix-all-button-is-not-presented-using-the-macos-system" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104713</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed an assertion that occurred when `HDCamera.allowDynamicResolution` was set to `true` via a script while using STP. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/assertion-failed-error-in-hdrp-builds-when-enabling-stp-via-script" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105731</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed an issue by significantly minimize an unwanted wobbly distortion when using HDRP's SSR with denoising. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/reflections-are-distorted-when-denoise-feature-is-enabled-for-screen-space-reflection-while-ray-tracing-is-active" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103202</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed an issue where an error was logged when using HDRP path tracer with PBR sky. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/compute-shader-property-not-set-error-when-entering-play-mode-with-path-tracing-and-pbr-sky" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-97700</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed an issue where changing the intensity of a realtime area light via script or animation wouldn't affect the color of the light. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/emissive-mesh-emissiveness-does-not-change-when-the-area-light-intensity-is-animated-through-script" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99991</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed an issue where HDRP water rendered always on top of high-quality-line-hair. Now you can use the LineRendering.CompositionMode = BeforeColorPyramidAfterClouds in the volume profile to fix this. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hair-is-not-rendered-with-water-physics-when-overlapping-with-another-gameobject" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110959</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed artifacts being visible as random bright tiles in HDRP deferred lighting after disabling path tracing. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/raytracing-renders-artefacts-and-performance-drops-when-switching-volumes-at-runtime" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112725</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed Decal Projector Layer Mask Override UI. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdrp-decal-projector-rendering-layer-mask-overwrite-hidden-when-the-value-is-changed" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104831</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed default fog volume shader warning. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdrp-slash-defaultfogvolume-implicit-truncation-of-vector-type-warning-is-thrown-when-building-hdrp-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105000</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed GC Allocation in Lens Flare Post Processing effect. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/per-frame-gc-allocation-when-using-hdrp-lens-flare-post-process-effect" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108846</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed ghosting artifacts from particles when using HDRP's SSR with denoising enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/moving-vfx-particles-leave-a-ghosting-trial-in-ray-traced-screen-space-reflections" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103766</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed missing asset icon for HDRP asset after creating it while typing its name. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urp-asset-has-no-icon-when-creating-a-new-urp-asset-and-is-not-selected-after-creation" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114896</a>)

-   HDRP: Fixed SSR debug modes causing an exception when they are enabled.

-   HDRP: Force fixed exposure while scene view filtering is active to avoid over-exposure and ensure highlighted objects are visible. (HDRP-3312)

-   HDRP: Improved HDRP's ReBLUR denoiser to longer hold on to stale samples (for example causing the appearance of black outlines). (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/objects-receive-a-black-outline-in-ray-traced-screen-space-reflections" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103701</a>)

-   HDRP: Reload Window fails to reload the HDRP Wizard window. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdrp-wizard-reload-window-fails-to-reload-the-hdrp-wizard-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104714</a>)

-   HLSLcc: Fixed HLSL countBits function incorrectly translated to GLSL. (UUM-62653)

-   IL2CPP: Changed the serialized form of GenericContainerIndex in the global metadata file to use the smallest possible numeric representation, reducing the metadata size for most programs.

-   IL2CPP: Changed the serialized form of ParameterIndex in the global metadata file to use the smallest possible numeric representation, reducing the metadata size for most programs

-   IL2CPP: Fixed a crash that occurred when extracting parameter information from a method with a nullable enum parameter that had a default value. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/macos-ios-il2cpp-crash-when-using-the-nullable-enum-as-a-parameter-and-passing-a-default-value-into-it" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116854</a>)

-   IL2CPP: Fixed an issue where IL2CPP randomly crashing with exit code 139 on macOS arm64. (UUM-125068)

-   IL2CPP: Fixed an issue where logs were sometimes scrambled in Android's logcat when using `Console.WriteLine`. (UUM-114863)

-   IL2CPP: Fixed issue to ensure macOS `.dSYM` debug symbol bundles are reliably copied to the build output on clean builds when detailed stacktraces are enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/dsym-file-is-not-created-during-macos-standalone-build" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108362</a>)

-   IL2CPP: Fixed issue where Linux was running out of memory pages due to them not being returned. (UUM-110285)

-   IL2CPP: Fixed issue where some references type values only stored in a struct field would not be reported in the memory profiler. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/memory-profiler-does-not-report-struct-types-when-they-are-used-in-static-fields-in-il2cpp-scripting-backend" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99608</a>)

-   IL2CPP: Fixed possible debug assert when a delegate type has a generic struct parameter when script debugging is enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/assertion-fail-when-running-a-script-debugging-build-from-an-exported-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110446</a>)

-   IL2CPP: Fixed XML Deserialization of a byte array leaving the reader at the wrong element. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/xml-data-is-incorrectly-parsed-due-to-xmlreader-dot-bytearraytohex-when-deserializing-in-webgl-builds" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105294</a>)

-   IL2CPP: Fixed: Prevent possible shutdown deadlock when using waits with zero time out. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/player-with-il2cpp-scripting-backend-crashes-when-calling-application-dot-quit-while-a-thread-is-running" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104910</a>)

-   IL2CPP: Runtime of il2cpp reduced on macOS Arm64 but building il2cpp for nativeaot. (UUM-108129)

-   IMGUI: Fixed the fallback font weight for Roboto Mono in IMGUI. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/monospaced-fonts-do-not-maintain-a-fixed-width-when-using-the-bold-variants" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110337</a>)

-   Input: Fixed issue where the cursor was not showing on Windows builds with Splashscreen disabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/custom-cursor-doesnt-show-in-build-when-the-splash-screen-is-disabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111052</a>)

-   Input System: Added missing support for normalised or raw scroll wheel values for macOS (OSX). (UUM-90425)

-   Input System: Fixed an issue that prevented the Unity Editor or Player from quitting when the Input System polling frequency was very low. (UUM-115335)

-   Input System: Fixed double cursor showing when using a custom texture for cursor while leaving and returning to the application. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/cursormode-dot-forcesoftware-displays-both-in-game-and-os-level-cursors-when-the-cursor-is-dragged-out-and-brought-back-to-the-standalone-build-and-the-project-is-using-the-new-input-system" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111925</a>)

-   Input System: Fixed incorrect HID device initial data until the HID device sends an input report. (UUM-120732)

-   Input System: Fixed mouse warp not working correctly on macOS since it would not update Mouse position state. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-mouse-position-is-not-updated-when-the-mouse-dot-warpcursorposition-function-is-executed" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121296</a>)

-   Input System: Fixed resize cursor issues in a windowed standalone Player build when using the Input System. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/cursor-remains-in-resizing-state-when-hovering-over-the-player-edges-and-fullscreen-mode-is-set-to-windowed-with-resizable-window-option-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117813</a>)

-   Input System: Fixed: Resolved issue with the Siri Remote (Gen2) where navigating with the Directional Pad caused `buttonSouth` to become true. This was due to OS propogating A button events after a D-Pad click (rather than a swipe). (UUM-110170)

-   Input System: The back and forward buttons on Windows were switched when their state was read in the Input System. The issue is now fixed. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/mouse-forward-is-detected-when-mouse-backward-is-pressed-or-held" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105141</a>)

-   Input System: When warping the mouse position with the Input System, the user needed to physically move the mouse to update to the new position. This issue is fixed. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/mouse-dot-warpcursorposition-does-not-warp-cursor-position-when-used-with-cursor-dot-forcesoftware" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108647</a>)

-   Installer: Fixed a potential exploit in the Windows Editor Installer.

-   Installer: Removed Visual C++ 2013 Redistributables from Windows Editor Installer. (UUM-104797)

-   iOS: Do not reset input if requested orientation change is the same as previous (no actual change). (UUM-114722)

-   iOS: Fix build failure when using Beta version of Xcode 26 with Metal Toolchain installed. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-beta-building-app-fails-when-metal-toolchain-26-dot-0-is-installed" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111730</a>)

-   iOS: Fix Frameworks folder appearing red in an exported Xcode project (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-exported-xcode-unity-iphone-xcode-project-shows-the-framework-folder-as-red" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108336</a>)

-   iOS: Fixed an issue related to changing the default audio output device while recording from the built-in microphone. In that case, sometimes microphone data would stop being captured properly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-audiosource-errors-slash-warning-logged-and-audiosettings-dot-onaudioconfigurationchanged-event-not-called-when-disconnecting-the-bluetooth-heaphones-from-the-device" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-98507</a>)

-   iOS: Fixed an issue where Canvas was not being rendered when app goes to background. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-canvas-elements-disappear-when-an-app-is-put-in-the-app-switcher-when-the-canvas-has-screenspace-camera-set-as-the-render-mode" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114602</a>)

-   iOS: Fixed an issue where the touch keyboard on iOS reported it was visible after dismissal. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-the-touchscreenkeyboard-dot-visible-reports-true-when-the-on-screen-keyboard-is-hidden" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121639</a>)

-   iOS: Fixed arg typo in updateLayerDrawableSizeFromBounds. (UUM-125390)

-   iOS: Fixed Build & Run for iOS,tvOS,visionOS platforms. Xcode project is closed properly now.

-   iOS: Fixed Frameworks directory appearing red when there are no custom frameworks. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-exported-xcode-unity-iphone-xcode-project-shows-the-framework-folder-as-red" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108336</a>)

-   iOS: Fixed occasional crashes when using GPU batched skinning. (UUM-117690)

-   iOS: Fixed warning in the Xcode console when keyboard is open and changing orientation on iPad. (UUM-95530)

-   iOS: Improved keyboard input field toolbar positioning by using inputAccessoryView, especially for floating keyboards. (UUM-100139)

-   Kernel: Fixed a crash occurring when using TransformAccessArray.SetTransforms with a Transform and then swapping this Transform for a RectTransform. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-transformhierarchychangedispatch-dispatchselfandallchildren-when-entering-play-mode-after-setting-the-source-image-field-of-the-image-component" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-79005</a>)

-   Kernel: Fixed rare deadlock on Windows in `Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets`. (UUM-100691)

-   Kernel: Fixed: JobHandle.Complete() goes to sleep sooner when there is no work to steal. This allows the waiting thread to give up the CPU core sooner to allow another thread to perform work or to preserve battery life. (UUM-83082)

-   Kernel: Upgraded TinyXml2 from version 9.0.0 to 11.0.0. (UUM-108036)

-   License: Bundle Licensing Client 1.17.2. (UUM-113543)

-   Linux: Fixed Linux runtime not respecting popupwindow argument. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/desktop-linux-runtime-does-not-respect-popupwindow-command-line-argument" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105221</a>)

-   Linux: Fixed Linux runtime window unexpectedly resizes to monitor dimensions when calling "Screen.SetResolution" function with hardcoded resolution matching current display's width or height (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-player-window-unexpectedly-resizes-to-monitor-dimensions-when-calling-screen-dot-setresolution-function-with-hardcoded-resolution-matching-current-displays-width-or-height" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107466</a>)

-   Linux: Fixed Steam Deck D-Pad is either not working or sometimes working when UI->Navigation is set to "Pass Through" in Input Actions. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-d-pad-is-either-not-working-or-sometimes-working-when-ui-navigation-is-set-to-pass-through-in-input-actions" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-97138</a>)

-   macOS: Added a warning dialog to alert users that all files will be deleted when building Apple platforms using the Replace option.  
    Added restricted directories when building Apple platforms. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/files-in-the-target-folder-are-deleted-without-a-proper-warning-when-building-an-ios-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109815</a>)

-   macOS: Create an universal .dSYM in build folder when building Intel+Silicon build (UUM-112044)

-   macOS: Fixed GPU occlusion culling interop with MSAA rt. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-the-renderer-becomes-invisible-or-flickers-when-gpu-occlusion-culling-is-enabled-and-ios-version-16-and-newer-devices-are-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-87043</a>)

-   macOS: Fixed Local Network permissions being reused for multiple Unity apps. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/macos-network-permission-shows-the-name-of-previously-closed-app-when-different-app-requests-network-access" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108681</a>)

-   macOS: Fixed mouse delta spike when cursor is set to the center of the screen (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-camera-abruptly-jumps-after-resuming-the-game-when-the-mouse-is-moved-slightly" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112550</a>)

-   Mono: Fixed a `ref` parameter address mismatch when invoking a default interface method with multiple `ref` arguments. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ref-parameter-address-mismatch-when-invoking-a-default-interface-method-with-multiple-ref-arguments" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115835</a>)

-   Mono: Fixed bug where attaching a profiler could cause Unity to crash to desktop. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-mono-domain-jit-foreach-when-performing-various-unity-operations" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112001</a>)

-   Mono: Fixed crash on some default interface method calls. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-raiseexception-when-using-default-interface-methods" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99049</a>)

-   Mono: Fixed crash that would occur due to symbol collision between builtin Brotli compression and system installed versions. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/silicon-freeze-slash-crash-on-brotlidecoderdecompressstream-when-using-system-dot-io-dot-compression-dot-brotlidecoder-dot-trydecompress" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108000</a>)

-   Mono: Fixed Crash when invoking a default interface method that is generic with value type generic argument. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-mono-class-inflate-generic-method-full-checked-when-accessing-static-properties-or-methods-of-a-generic-interface-in-a-generic-class" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109706</a>)

-   Mono: Fixed GC Handle leak that would happen every time a new function pointer was returned for a managed delegate. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unmanagedreadorwrite-objects-are-not-disposed-off-when-gzipstream-is-used-in-the-using-statement" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105139</a>)

-   Mono: Fixed issue where mono's debugger agent thread would not restart when a connection issue was encountered. (UUM-107431)

-   Mono: Fixed the System.String.Replace throwing NotImplementedException. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/notimplementedexception-is-thrown-when-using-system-dot-string-dot-replace-string-string-boolean-cultureinfo" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117367</a>)

-   Mono: Prevent crash when Method.MakeGenericMethod is called with invalid generic argument types. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-raiseexception-when-addgameelement-handles-a-generic-type" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109251</a>)

-   Mono: Protected calls to `fork` with a `gc_lock` on all macOS variations. (UUM-101541)

-   Mono: Re-added support for Jetbrains Rider profiling, which had been temporarily removed with a recent security fix. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/mono-profiling-in-jetbrains-rider-is-broken-as-a-result-of-october-3-2025-security-fix" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-126137</a>)

-   Multiplayer: Fixed "enable Multiplayer Roles" option being reset to false every time the project is open. (UUM-111695)

-   Multiplayer: Fixed an issue in Multiplayer Play mode where removing an active Scenario Configuration fails to close running instances. (MTT-12535)

-   Multiplayer: Fixed an issue where removing an Instance configuration from within the Configure Play Mode Scenarios window results in a null reference error.

-   Multiplayer: Fixed an issue where setting an active Multiplayer Play Mode scenario configuration with local simulated instances results in an SQP error. (MTT-13023)

-   Multiplayer: Fixed issue where a Local Sim instance within Multiplayer Scenarios can incorrectly show an idle state in the Play Mode Status window. (MTT-12921)

-   Multiplayer: Fixed scene hash does not exist and scene list issues in virtual players in Multiplayer Play Mode. (UUM-121576)

-   Multiplayer: Multiplayer: Notify users of Drift when assets or code changes within the Main Editor starts diverging from free running instances.

-   N/A (internal): Fixed missing reflection probes variants in player builds with GPUResidentDrawer.

-   N/A (internal): Fixed: Reverted fields to properties back to fix compatibility for precompiled assemblies. (UUM-107276)

-   Networking: Updated our copy of MbedTLS to version 3.6.4 to address some security vulnerabilities. (UUM-114079)

-   Package: Version Control: Fixed obsolete references to TreeViewState (VCS-1007414)

-   Package Manager: Fix package manager links so that they follow the WCAG contrast guidelines (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/package-manager-light-mode-links-fail-wcag-contrast-guidelines" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-78610</a>)

-   Package Manager: Fix the issue where "Add package by name" dropdown shows up different when opening through the Package Manager and through the web link. (UUM-83727)

-   Package Manager: Fixed an issue where extension buttons for installed packages in the Package Manager were not consistently displayed. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/inconsistent-logic-on-when-open-vfx-graph-samples-button-appears-on-visual-effect-graph-in-package-manager" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-84399</a>)

-   Package Manager: Fixed an issue where installing a git package using Git LFS would not work with Git LFS 3.6.0 and higher. (UUM-91342)

-   Package Manager: Fixed an issue where Package Manager action buttons overlapped when the window width was small. (UUM-108911)

-   Package Manager: Fixed so now GetPackagesPath are thread/serialization safe. (UUM-104998)

-   Package Manager: Fixed the issue where Assets cannot be imported when the Asset package contains project settings and all project settings are unchanged. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/assets-do-not-get-imported-from-an-asset-package-when-library-assets-in-the-asset-package-are-unchanged" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-100078</a>)

-   Package Manager: Fixed the issue where Project Settings cannot be imported when the Asset package contains project settings and all Assets are unchanged. (UUM-90627)

-   Package Manager: Fixed the issue where scoped registry url validation message is different when user try to add or update a scoped registry. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/url-validation-message-in-scoped-registries-is-unclear-and-lacks-informative-details" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113060</a>)

-   Package Manager: Fixed the issue where the details panel is too width when text starts cropping. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/package-description-text-is-cut-off-when-resizing-unity-package-manager-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107297</a>)

-   Package Manager: Fixed the issue where the `Reset` button and the `Customized` icon have different logics and show up at different times. (UUM-82875)

-   Package Manager: Fixed the issue where there are invalid Unity major versions in the inspector dropdown. (UUM-85694)

-   Package Manager: Removed retry option from PackageManager error during project creation. (UUM-111460)

-   Package Manager: The sign in button should always be visible when user is signed out and package list view is at low width. (UUM-104872)

-   Package Manager: Updated missing or invalid signature warnings and errors so they're now logged only when adding or updating a package, instead of during every resolve.

-   Particles: Avoid throwing errors when a trail renderer is auto-destructed while using the trail preview in the editor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/errors-are-thrown-when-a-gameobject-with-trail-renderer-is-selected-in-the-hierarchy-moved-and-destroyed" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109475</a>)

-   Particles: Changed default particle material for HDRP to be consistent with other pipelines (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/particle-system-renders-squares-instead-of-circles-in-scene-and-game-view-when-created-in-hdrp-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113852</a>)

-   Particles: Document unexpected behavior for PauseAndCatchUp culling mode (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/particle-system-isnt-playing-in-play-mode-when-its-out-of-view-in-scene-view-simulation-space-is-set-to-world-and-culling-mode-is-set-to-pause-and-catch-up" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102085</a>)

-   Particles: Fix jittering of particle editor when the window is made too small (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/particle-effect-window-tab-slider-is-jittery-and-not-smooth-when-resizing-the-docked-particle-effect-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105164</a>)

-   Particles: Fixed a potential crash that occurred when calling `GetParticles` in a particle system with trails. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-the-player-crashes-after-a-few-seconds-when-particles-are-generated-and-deleted-continuously-over-time" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-40536</a>)

-   Particles: Fixed an issue where some buttons overlapped in the Particle System editor window when the window size was too small. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/particle-effect-window-buttons-overlap-with-other-ui-elements-when-opened-in-editor" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105168</a>)

-   Particles: Fixed crash when using a speed multiplier that takes value of 0 and velocity is used to select the sprite. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-generateparticlegeometry-when-creating-certain-particles" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111657</a>)

-   Particles: Fixed particle system tooltips where shown in the inspector, even though the UI controls were not visible. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/particle-system-property-description-labels-show-through-the-particle-systems-curve-editor-when-hovering-the-mouse-over-the-curve-editor" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-100925</a>)

-   Particles: fixed to prevent TempJob memory leak warning when playing Particle Systems in Edit mode. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-writeparticlelinevertex-when-particle-systems-are-rendered-in-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103773</a>)

-   Particles: Particles are now filling UNITY_MATRIX_PREV_M with identity to be consistent with world matrix. (UUM-114886)

-   Physics: Ensure havok shows up as "Experimental" when enabled. (PHYS-439)

-   Physics: Fixed a crash inside Physics.IgnoreCollision where a user would pass a prefab asset (not a prefab asset instance) to the API thus internally ending with Transform components that don't belong to a UnityScene. An error message will now be issued when this usage is executed. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-in-physicsmanager-ignorecollision-when-a-gameobject-with-is-trigger-enabled-enters-a-collision-in-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116541</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed a crash that occurred when calling `Rigidbody.Move` on a disabled `Rigidbody` component. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-physx-scb-body-setkinematictarget-when-moving-disabled-parent-kinematic-rigidbody" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-126746</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed a crash that would occur when adding a Cloth component to a GameObject with an existing TextMesh component. By default both Cloth and TextMesh components can't share the same GameObject due to the fact that they require different renderers to work, MeshRenderer and SkinnedMeshRenderer respectively. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editor-crashes-on-pptr-after-adding-text-mesh-and-cloth-components-to-the-same-gameobject" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-125416</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed a crash when exiting playmode, which was caused due to physics scene data not being disposed in a thread safe manner. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-cleanupafterload-when-exiting-play-mode-during-async-scene-load-slash-unload-events" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-92619</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed a crash with regards to joint gizmo drawing where having a disabled ArticulationBody as a Joint component's connected body would provide invalid data. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-getjointcurrentactortransforms-when-selecting-a-gameobject-with-specific-configurable-joints" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114481</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed a memory leak that would occur when resizing the internal contacts array for physics scenes (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/memory-usage-increases-each-time-the-meshcollider-component-is-turned-off-and-on" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112248</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed a number of crashes due to access to physics scene data not being thread safe when loading a large number of scenes. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-when-a-scene-is-created-with-localphysicsmode-physics3d" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-71174</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed an issue where async component destruction would corrupt the physx vehicle integration, effectively breaking filtering data for wheels. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/wheel-collider-does-not-interact-with-other-colliders-when-the-gameobject-is-destroyed-and-instantiated" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117450</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed an issue where setting geometry data to GeometryHolder would write out of bounds, due the the memory alignment being incorrect on 32bit builds.

-   Physics: Fixed an issue where when creating a high number of tree colliders on terrain, the editor would crash. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-windowsvideomedia-getvideoframeatindex-when-instantiating-a-prefab-with-rigidbody-after-loading-a-scene-from-an-asset-bundle" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108799</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed an issue with pinning particles of a Cloth component at runtime from script or using authoring tools during playmode. (PHYS-669)

-   Physics: Fixed issue causing cloth to stretch significantly, especially with higher cloth resolutions. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/cloth-stretches-significantly-more-with-a-higher-vertex-count-even-when-stretch-stiffness-is-set-to-1" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116109</a>)

-   Physics: Fixed Multi-box pruning broad phase now restored, because automatic box pruning was insufficient for larger worlds. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/performance-dropping-when-using-automatic-box-prunning-or-sweep-and-prune-instead-of-multibox-pruning-broadphase" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112553</a>)

-   Physics: Resolved a NullReferenceException error in the Ragdoll Builder Window that occurred when clicking the 'Create' or 'Cancel' buttons. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-error-is-thrown-when-creating-a-ragdoll" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112209</a>)

-   Physics 2D: Added to the scripting documentation for all physics queries that return multiple results a note of "NOTE: All results are sorted into ascending distance order i.e. the first result is the closest." (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/2d-physics-queries-do-not-state-that-results-are-sorted-into-ascending-distance-order" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120631</a>)

-   Physics 2D: Ensure that "Rigidbody2D.Slide" correctly ignores colliders specified with "Physics2D.IgnoreCollision". (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/rigidbody2d-dot-slide-correctly-ignores-colliders-specified-with-physics2d-dot-ignorecollision-however-encountering-them-can-still-modify-movement-behaviour-in-subtle-ways" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120474</a>)

-   Physics 2D: Fixed an issue where an uninitialized normal impulse for a contact is returned. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/contactpoint2d-dot-normalimpulse-returns-a-high-value-when-touching-the-wall-with-the-object-at-rest-and-multithreading-enabled-for-physics2d" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112903</a>)

-   Physics 2D: Fixed an issue with `Rigidbody2D` linear and angular drag to damping rename when used in prefab variants. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/prefab-variants-do-not-keep-the-values-of-linear-drag-and-angular-drag-while-upgrading-the-project-from-unity-6000-dot-0" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122242</a>)

-   Physics 2D: Fixed memory leak when reporting 2D physics collision callbacks. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/memory-leak-when-using-rigidbody2d-physics-in-webgl" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108093</a>)

-   Physics 2D: Fixed Rigidbody2D.Slide should always move to the point of contact independent of the current slide angle threshold. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/rigidbody2d-dot-slide-doesnt-always-complete-a-movement-under-certain-circumstances" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120297</a>)

-   Physics 2D: Fixed tooltip rendering for the Rigidbody2D Simulation Mode layer selection in the Physics2DSettings. (UUM-105521)

-   Physics 2D: Fixed: Ensured that changing the Transform Z position only doesn't affect Rigidbody2D interpolation. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/changing-transform-dot-position-dot-z-per-frame-should-not-affect-rigidbody2d-interpolation" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108922</a>)

-   Playables: Fixed an issue where PlayableOutput.SetSourcePlayable would take a long time on PlayableGraphs with many outputs. (UUM-108822)

-   Player: Fixed an issue where the startup logs would be missing from the normal log file (or standard output) when the "Capture Startup Logs" player setting was enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/startup-logs-are-missing-in-player-logs-when-capture-startup-logs-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102670</a>)

-   Player: Fixed issues when setting the same value multiple times to developerConsoleEnabled.

-   Player: Fixed missing "device.gpu.driver" field in Dashboard (UUM-109887)

-   Player: Fixed: The DeveloperConsole now correctly opens after being re-enabled, fixing an issue where it would remain stuck in a closed state. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/debug-console-does-not-reappear-when-disabling-and-re-enabling-debug-dot-developerconsoleenabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109718</a>)

-   Player: The Player no longer becomes unresponsive after resolution switch in Exclusive Fullscreen mode when using the DX12 Graphics API. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-player-becomes-unresponsive-after-resolution-switch-in-exclusive-fullscreen-mode-when-using-dx12-graphics-api" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104762</a>)

-   Player: The Player now restores correctly when using Alt+Tab in the Exclusive Fullscreen mode when using the DX12 Graphics API. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/dx12-exclusive-full-screen-mode-player-window-sometimes-doesnt-restore-correctly-after-using-alt-plus-tab" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-67373</a>)

-   Plugins: AMDUnityPlugin/FSR2: Fixed white flickering around the edges of the image when the camera is rotated. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/white-edges-appear-during-camera-movement-in-runtime-when-using-fsr2-with-motion-vectors-and-exposure-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110296</a>)

-   Prefabs: Fixed a crash occurring when opening a scene with missing prefabs and added components in prefab modifications. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-deleteprefabinstanceobjectsformissingprefabs-when-opening-a-specific-project-for-the-first-time" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122108</a>)

-   Prefabs: Fixed a crash that was occurring when loading a scene containing an unlinked prefab that had an AddedComponent prefab modification. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-gameobject-removecomponentfromgameobjectinternal-when-opening-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105792</a>)

-   Prefabs: Fixed an error when creating a new script while adding a new component to a prefab via the AddComponent context menu. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/error-when-adding-valid-monobehaviour-script-to-prefab-in-project-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111102</a>)

-   Prefabs: Fixed issue where m_Modifications block serializes incorrectly when replacing source prefabs. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/m-modifications-block-serializes-incorrectly-when-replacing-source-prefabs" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107408</a>)

-   Profiler: Fixed "Profiler is not able to flush data to a file or socket" warning after auto connecting Profiler to a Player on OSX (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/profiler-is-not-able-to-flush-data-to-a-file-or-socket-warning-thrown-after-auto-connecting-profiler-to-build-create" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112860</a>)

-   Profiler: Fixed align profiler frame duration of Edit mode profiling with Play mode when playing. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-profiler-ignores-a-modified-targetframerate-when-profiling-edit-mode" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-74665</a>)

-   Profiler: Fixed FrameDataView.GetFrameMetaData api return data after FrameDataView.GetSampleMetaData api call. (UUM-119613)

-   Profiler: Fixed FrameTiming.cpuMainThreadFrameTime and Profiler to not include RenderThread wait times when Split Graphics Jobs are enabled. (UUM-104394)

-   Profiler: Fixed Highlights module and/or frame range selection resetting on domain reload.

-   Profiler: Fixed invalid Mesh indices appearing in Memory Profiler metadata when read/write is disabled on standalone builds. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/mesh-object-has-an-invalid-indices-value-in-a-memory-snapshot-of-a-player-build-when-the-read-slash-write-option-of-the-mesh-is-disabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107356</a>)

-   Profiler: Fixed issue with "Target Frames Per Second" Profiler setting having inconsistent results when entering an overly large/small value. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/entering-a-big-number-into-the-target-frames-per-second-in-profilers-preferences-inconsistently-converts-it-either-to-1fps-or-1000fps" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116904</a>)

-   Profiler: Fixed Player hang during Autoconnect on long Player initialization. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/player-unresponsive-when-building-a-specific-project-with-autoconnect-profiler-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-101218</a>)

-   Profiler: Fixed serialization system memory attribution to MonoScript and global caches (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/visualtreeasset-has-significantly-increased-memory-allocation-size-at-runtime-when-the-project-is-built-with-il2cpp-scripting-backend" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110303</a>)

-   QNX: Fixed main thread name setting. (UUM-116884)

-   QNX: Fixed Vulkan Multidisplay Crash. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-urp-vulkan-player-crash-when-activating-a-second-display-with-a-second-camera-targeting-it" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-101885</a>)

-   QNX: Updated `Screen.SetResolution` to correctly resize the window buffer in windowed mode on QNX. (UUM-114197)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed a bug where an overlay docked in a corner would be directly undocked when quickly clicking on the dragger. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/overlays-are-immediately-undocked-when-the-overlay-is-docked-to-the-right-side-of-scene-view-and-the-overlays-handle-is-clicked" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116551</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed a bug where Camera overlays dropdown wouldn't display the camera name. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/scene-view-cameras-overlay-shows-no-camera-found-when-main-camera-exists-in-the-scene" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104633</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed a bug where renaming an EditorTool would throw an error message. (UUM-113403)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed a bug where the folding out overlays and change them to toolbar mode would only show their grabber.

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed a bug where the Overlay Menu would not be usable when the window does not have active overlays. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/sprite-editor-overlay-menu-issues" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113611</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed a bug where the overlays would be offset from the top of the SceneView when dragged. (UUM-109220)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed an issue where an Overlay could have an empty title and be shown as an empty button in the overlay menu toolbar. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/no-default-icon-for-the-custom-overlay-without-the-defined-icon-in-the-overlay-menu" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114575</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed an issue where Focus Pan command would often set an impractical zoom pivot if Canvas object was picked. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/scene-view-doesnt-select-the-canvas-when-its-clicked-with-the-view-tool" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108790</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed an issue where opening the Game view caused a crash if the Editor layout had previously been serialized with an invalid display target value. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-guimanager-doguievent-when-focusing-on-the-game-view-window-on-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115918</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed an issue where the Camera first person mode in the Cameras overlay wasn't clickable after restarting the Editor with the Game view focused. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-camera-first-person-mode-in-cameras-overlay-is-greyed-out-and-not-clickable-when-the-editor-is-restarted-with-the-game-view-focused" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108801</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed an issue where the editor would throw if layers StateCache json file was empty. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/error-nullreferenceexception-object-reference-not-set-to-an-instance-of-an-object-is-present-when-opening-the-project-or-reloading-the-domain" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116877</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed an issue where the shortcut table's selection in Shortcut Manager would not update correctly when changing search queries. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/shortcut-is-set-for-the-wrong-function-when-changing-multiple-shortcuts" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-126160</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed issue where the overlays' menu popup would only open when UITK elements were hovered. (UUM-114670)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed Shortcut Manager's UI elements overlapping when window is resized down to narrow width. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-elements-overlap-in-the-shortcuts-window-when-docked-and-resized-to-a-smaller-window-size" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115947</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed the Cameras overlay popup having the wrong height when first person controls are toggled in the overlay. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/cameras-menu-window-size-is-broken-when-control-the-selected-camera-in-first-person-dot-is-selected" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110292</a>)

-   Scene/Game View: Fixed UI layout issue for Scene view's 3D Mode Placement preference. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/3d-placement-mode-dropdown-is-not-aligned-and-too-far-to-the-right-side-in-the-scene-view-tab-in-preferences-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122127</a>)

-   Scripting: Disallowed RuntimeInitializeOnLoad decorated methods in generic types (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/invalidoperationexception-thrown-when-in-standalone-build-a-runtimeinitializeonloadmethod-is-defined-in-an-open-generic-type" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110409</a>)

-   Scripting: Disallowed RuntimeInitializeOnLoad decorated methods in generic types. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/invalidoperationexception-thrown-when-in-standalone-build-a-runtimeinitializeonloadmethod-is-defined-in-an-open-generic-type" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110409</a>)

-   Scripting: Fixed failure to enter playmode when assembly reload is locked and script changes are pending. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/entering-play-mode-fails-when-using-editorapplication-dot-lockreloadassemblies-with-imported-changes-to-the-c-number-script" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114845</a>)

-   Scripting: Fixed GameView not rendering on Quest when hidden in Editor (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/quest-game-view-is-no-longer-rendered-in-the-hmd-if-its-hidden-in-the-editor-when-using-meta-link" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119451</a>)

-   Scripting: Fixed ObjectPool clear timing in no domain reload environment. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/objectpools-pool-is-destroyed-when-entering-play-mode-without-reloading-domain" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111093</a>)

-   Scripting: Fixed possible managed memory corruption when using API's that use arrays as output buffers (UUM-123044)

-   Scripting: Fixed so that systems performing argument validation (Infinity, NaN etc) also do so in a Player Development Build. (UUM-105153)

-   Scripting: Fixed UnityEvent not executing runtime callbacks in some circumstances. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unity-events-added-to-a-scriptableobject-are-not-invoked-if-they-are-added-in-edit-mode-and-their-call-state-is-set-to-runtime-only" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-73759</a>)

-   Scripting: Fixed: Optimized the Main Thread times of InstantiateAsync and added a new OriginalImmutable flag to reduce the times even more. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/long-scheduler-times-on-main-thread-when-using-instantiateasync-with-a-singular-massive-prefab" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102307</a>)

-   Scripting: Fixed: Prevent recursion when unloading Scriptable Object. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-tlsf-free-when-calling-unloadresources-with-the-ondisable-function" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-77941</a>)

-   Scripting: RoslynAnalyzersShouldNotReferenceNetStandard2_1 test instability fixed. (UUM-110692)

-   Search: Changed the default operator for the filter "dir" from "equals" to "contains". (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/dir-search-proposition-uses-equals-as-its-default-comparison-operator" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110821</a>)

-   Search: Fixed focus and navigation issues in Search's QueryBuilder. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/focus-issue-between-text-query-block-and" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110828</a>)

-   Search: Fixed: QueryMarkers correctly supports strings with quotes. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/searchexpression-strips-quotes" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108372</a>)

-   Search: Fixed: Support interface filtering for component and asset types. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/search-filtering-by-interface-type-does-not-work" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109613</a>)

-   Serialization: Added additional checks to better catch merging errors in UnityYAML. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editorutility-dot-isvalidunityyaml-failing-to-properly-validate-yaml-parsed-into-it" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-98013</a>)

-   Serialization: Fixed an issue where mixed values might not appear correctly in the inspector after using Undo. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/inspector-field-does-not-revert-to-mixed-value-on-undo-when-new-value-matches-the-second-objects-original-value" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109513</a>)

-   Serialization: Fixed: The `[SerializeField]` attribute is now marked with `[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Field)]`, preventing people from applying it to things that aren't fields, e.g. because they got mixed up between `[SerializeField]` and `[Serializable]`. Any code that was applying it to things other than fields will now produce compiler errors; in these situations the attribute was doing nothing, so you can delete it without changing the behaviour of your code. (Thanks, spiney199!). (UUM-105742)

-   Services: Fixed exception events not appearing on the Diagnostics dashboard. (ULO-7366)

-   Shadergraph: Added SrcBlendAlpha and DstBlendAlpha properties if allowMaterialOverride is set to true on URP. (UUM-105226)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed an issue when deleting an open shader graph but canceling closure would result in a null reference when attempting to reclose it. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-is-logged-when-cancelling-shader-graph-recovery-of-an-already-deleted-shader-graph" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110302</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed an issue when opening generated shader, from the ShaderGraph asset's Inspector, that causes the first additional shader, if any exist, to be skipped from being piped to a temporary file for viewing.

-   Shadergraph: Fixed an issue where custom interpolator project settings were not clamping correctly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/enabling-override-variant-limit-toggle-warning-threshold-value-gets-set-to-8" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113366</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed an issue where nodes without valid previews would expand on creation and collapse on selection. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/empty-custom-function-node-is-created-expanded-and-then-shrinks-when-moved" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104202</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed extra scrollbars sometimes appearing in ShaderGraph blackboard when dragging attributes. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vertical-and-horizontal-scrollbars-appear-and-disappear-when-dragging-an-attribute-to-a-different-position-within-the-shader-graph-hierarchy" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108024</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed shadergraph compilation issues related to the Spacewarp support. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vulkan-the-shader-compiler-shows-an-undeclared-identifier-basemap-st-error-when-compiling-the-a-specific-shader" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108902</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed: Added wrapping to the custom function node's label and fixed an issue where it would not update immediately. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/custom-function-nodes-label-and-name-field-is-not-limited-to-the-graph-inspectors-bounds" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103861</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed: Float node documentation link now resolves correctly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/a-404-page-is-opened-when-selecting-open-documentation-on-the-float-node-in-shader-graph" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103781</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed: Shader Graph Asset help button now redirects to the correct URL. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/shader-graph-importers-documentation-reference-button-leads-to-a-404-page" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103777</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed: Speed Tree subgraphs documentation link now resolves correctly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/a-404-page-is-opened-when-selecting-open-documentation-for-any-speedtree-sub-graph-asset" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103972</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed: Sub Graph Asset help button now redirects to the correct URL. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/shader-graph-import-settings-documentation-leads-to-sorry-dot-dot-dot-that-page-seems-to-be-missing-page-when-opened-through-the-documentation-button-in-the-inspector-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105173</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Fixed: Sub Graph node documentation link now redirects to the correct URL. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/a-404-page-is-opened-when-selecting-open-documentation-for-any-custom-sub-graph-in-shader-graph" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103971</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Improved appearance of subgraph node display names in various areas. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/non-localized-labels-particleflipbookblending-and-softparticles-appear-in-the-ui" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111531</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Nodes are now properly selected after copy-paste or duplication. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nodes-in-shader-graph-are-not-selected-when-they-are-duplicated" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110841</a>)

-   Shadergraph: Preview time is now set to match scene time. (UUM-113807)

-   Shaders: Fixed a crash that could occur when opening projects with compute shaders.

-   Shaders: Fixed a rare assertion about keyword space mismatch happening when calling Material.IsKeywordEnabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/state-comes-from-an-incompatible-keyword-space-errors-when-building-entity-catalogs" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105534</a>)

-   Shaders: Fixed a regression issue when triggering keyword space mismatches on certain cases with UsePass and Fallback. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/state-comes-from-an-incompatible-keyword-space-error-when-clicking-on-a-shader" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122608</a>)

-   Shaders: Fixed CPU side performance regression when shader has dynamic branch keywords. (UUM-114974)

-   Shaders: Fixed editor crash occurring often with a freshly created project. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-tlsf-free-when-adding-a-3d-object-to-a-scene-of-a-new-universal-3d-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107673</a>)

-   Shaders: Fixed issue where user-defined constant buffer names containing substring "Globals" would fail in glslang-dependent compilation. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/shader-compile-process-adds-shader-id-to-the-constant-buffer-name-when-the-word-globals-is-being-used-in-vulkan" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120318</a>)

-   Shaders: Fixed stage specific shader keyword being an undeclared identifier when using Vulkan. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/stage-specific-shader-keywords-triggering-errors-on-vulkan-editor" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114398</a>)

-   Shaders: Fixed unnecessary shader warnings and errors being logged when running with -batchmode -nographics. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unnecessary-warnings-are-logged-when-running-player-with-batchmode-nographics" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114187</a>)

-   Shaders: Update UnityInput.hlsl to remove incorrect comment about time since level load.

-   SpeedTree: Fixed an issue where SpeedTrees loaded as asset bundles could cause the player to crash on exit. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-treerenderer-clearrenderers-when-exiting-built-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113224</a>)

-   SRP Core: Added OpenGLES3 to the list of XR systems that use texture arrays. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/rendergraph-xr-imported-textures-are-not-using-the-correct-dimension-and-slices" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104000</a>)

-   SRP Core: Dispose of compiled graphs on cleanup. (UUM-117495)

-   SRP Core: Fix invisible grid lines in Render Graph Viewer with light theme (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/separation-lines-are-missing-in-render-graph-wiever-window-between-access-blocks-in-light-theme" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113505</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed a misaligned Histogram Percentages in a exposure volume component for HDRP. (UUM-91672)

-   SRP Core: Fixed an Issue where the MaterialUpgrader in URP wouldnt enable Alpha Clipping on Materials that started out using Render Mode Cutout. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urp-material-upgrade-utility-does-not-enable-alpha-clipping-when-material-had-rendering-mode-set-to-cutout" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99709</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed editor crash when importing an asset store package that sets active render pipeline to an invalid asset. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-rendermanager-checkrenderpipelinechanges-when-importing-2d-beginner-adventure-game-complete-project-into-a-new-2d-urp-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114721</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed incorrect merging of passes in Render Graph when a pass targets a texture that is sampled in previous pass. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/using-addcopypass-causes-an-incorrect-merging-of-passes" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112857</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed IndexOutOfRangeException when using a buffer more times than a texture. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/indexoutofrangeexception-is-thrown-in-nativepasscompiler-when-a-graphics-buffer-is-used-in-more-than-5-render-passes" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120616</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed MaterialUpgrader not saving the upgraded materials to disk, causing preview icons to not update. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/model-and-prefab-preview-icons-are-not-updated-after-upgrading-associated-materials-to-urp" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105393</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed Memoryless RenderGraph Textures so now can be casted to RTHandles in SetRenderFunction(). (UUM-96238)

-   SRP Core: Fixed NullReferenceException due to missing DebugState annotation when trying to use DebugUI.ObjectField. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-is-thrown-when-assigning-an-object-to-a-custom-debugui-dot-objectfield-in-rendering-debugger" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105490</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed prefab override UI and mixed value behaviour for Rendering Layers mask UI. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/rendering-layer-mask-is-not-highlighted-when-the-property-is-overriden" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110587</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed Render Graph pass merging algorithm when VRS is enabled using Shading Rate Image attachment. (UUM-102113)

-   SRP Core: Fixed RenderGraph.Cleanup() correctly clears RenderGraph resource pools.

-   SRP Core: Fixed Rendering Debugger panel splitter not being clamped properly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/rendering-debugger-window-sections-do-not-have-a-minimum-width-set-when-resizing-with-the-slider-in-the-middle-of-the-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105576</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed the asserts in *Rendering Debugger* > *Volumes* tab after selecting a component. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ubuntu-assertionexceptions-are-constantly-spammed-into-the-console-when-in-volume-of-rendering-debugger-camera-is-set-to-screencamera-or-scenecamera-and-any-component-is-chosen" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116573</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed Volume component documentation link. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/volume-component-documentation-link-is-missing" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102257</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed Volume Profile changes not getting saved after the Paste Settings context menu action. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/paste-settings-functionality-does-not-save-the-pasted-settings-in-volume-profile-when-reloading-the-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119513</a>)

-   SRP Core: Fixed: Prevent selection of Default Volume profile list elements. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/graphics-settings-expandable-fields-have-weird-selection-highlight-when-clicking-on-the-empty-spot-inside-the-settings" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103064</a>)

-   SRP Core: FixedDisplay Volume Profile context menu actions as disabled if the profile is empty. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/context-menu-actions-collapse-all-expand-all-enable-all-disable-all-remove-all-reset-all-do-nothing-in-volume-component" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115965</a>)

-   SRP Core: Graphics setting container warning for Rendering Debugger resources. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/warning-about-universalrenderpipelineglobalsettings-into-urp-samples-package" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111391</a>)

-   SRP Core: Optimize the TryMergeRenderPasses step in CompilerRenderGraph by implementing a cache for checking the NextPassTargetsTexture Pass Breaking reason (UUM-122102)

-   SRP Core: Prevent Missing Additional Camera warning when the project build. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/camera-main-camera-does-not-contain-an-additional-camera-data-component-dot-warning-is-thrown-when-building-a-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121897</a>)

-   SRP Core: Removed extra manual clear operation for textures imported to Render Graph and used first as render pass attachments. (UUM-109284)

-   SRP Core: Replace default constructors in the Volume Components by DisplayInfo attribute.

-   Terrain: Added missing selected icons for Terrain properties. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/terrain-property-icons-blends-in-when-selected-in-animation-window-with-the-light-editor-theme-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114855</a>)

-   Terrain: Corrected behavior of height painting into multiple terrain tiles whose vertical ranges do not overlap. Height values are now correctly clamped instead of wrapping. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-edge-of-the-terrain-is-breaking-when-painting-along-the-edges-where-the-two-terrains-connect" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105782</a>)

-   Terrain: Fixed a crash that could occur when preventing instanced details from being added to detail texture atlas. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-detaildatabase-setuppreloadtextureatlasdata-when-building-a-specific-scene" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122921</a>)

-   Terrain: Fixed low-resolution icons for high PPI and retina displays.

-   Terrain: Fixed: Overlays icon state for selected built-in Terrain tools.

-   Terrain: Removed unused options from the Shader Graph terrain subtarget and removed unused checkboxes from the Shader Graph terrain material user interface.

-   Test Framework: Fixed a stack overflow exception in the Test Runner UI that might happen with some assembly setups with precompiled assemblies. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/stackoverflowexception-occurs-and-tests-fail-to-load-when-the-test-runner-window-is-opened-while-system-dot-windows-dot-forms-dot-dll-and-system-dot-deployment-dot-dll-are-present-in-the-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122047</a>)

-   Tests: NVUnityPlugin & DLSS Debug View no longer allocates heap memory, which were causing the locally run HDP Tests DLSS tests to fail with heap memory usage detection.

-   Tests: Re-enabled and updated an integration test with build profiles and graphics settings. (UUM-84148)

-   Tests: Retrieve Android Cmdline and Build Tools versions based on AndroidSDKTools when getting Android Tools paths in Workspace class. (ANT-2957)

-   Tests: Reverted fence dependency checking algorithm changes. (UUM-116058)

-   Text: Add ATG Bitmap Support

-   Text: Add early out with invalid padding. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-editor-crashes-when-generating-font-atlas-in-the-font-asset-creator-with-9999999999-padding-and-256x256-atlas-resolution" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117619</a>)

-   Text: Add proper fallback for CJK on macOS 15 (UUM-92534)

-   Text: Ensure line-breaking rules are preserved for CJK (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/leading-bracket-is-left-behind-on-the-previous-line-when-text-wraps-in-textmeshpro" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108849</a>)

-   Text: Fixed a caching issue with fallback behavior. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/headerattribute-is-not-displayed-in-the-inspector-when-using-non-ascii-characters-in-the-headers-text" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115402</a>)

-   Text: Fixed IME issue. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-textmeshpro-input-field-gets-cleared-when-characters-are-entered-using-a-japanese-keyboard-layout-and-a-suggestion-is-selected-from-the-composition-string-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-100552</a>)

-   Text: Fixed issue to now ensure the Advanced Text Generator supports Text Auto Size. (UUM-105208)

-   Text: Fixed link tag vertical alignment issue. (UUM-113973)

-   Text: Fixed: Expose PostProcessTextVertices to enable text animation without the need for reflection. (UUM-108065)

-   TextCore: Ensured that disabled `TextField` components do not collapse when empty. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/text-field-becomes-narrow-when-selecting-children-of-toggle-element-in-ui-builder" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103184</a>)

-   TextCore: Fixed a thread issue with underline. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-toolkit-rendered-text-breaks-when-toggling-fontweight-styles-through-code" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-124858</a>)

-   TextCore: Fixed richTextTag not taking into account the display scaling. (UUM-104160)

-   TextCore: Init ClassDef vectors to prevent out-of-bounds crashes. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-textcore-otl-gpos-tablereader-getlookuplist-when-changing-to-a-specific-font-in-the-ui-builder" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109409</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Added incorrect selection of font asset fallback for a given character font weight and style. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/assigned-fonts-bold-slash-font-weight-styles-render-using-default-fonts-assets-when-default-font-has-corresponding-style-assets-defined-in-tmp-settings" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107331</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Fix iOS selection issue (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ios-clicking-input-field-does-not-always-select-the-whole-text-on-focus-when-hide-mobile-input-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112457</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Fixed an issue by revising style padding handling to exclude sprites. (UUM-102112)

-   TextMeshPro: Fixed Font Asset -> Glyph Adjustment Pair -> Options property not updating correctly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/character-spacing-value-is-not-applied-when-using-a-specific-font-asset" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-68242</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Fixed incorrect handling of ligatures that contains variant selectors in their input sequences. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/some-emoji-and-ligature-sequences-containing-variant-selectors-are-misinterpreted" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111750</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Fixed incorrect Ligature glyph atlas index when using font assets with Multi Atlas Texture enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/emojis-with-different-skin-tone-options-show-incorrectly-when-multi-atlas-textures-is-enabled-in-the-font-asset-properties" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111770</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Fixed incorrect preferred height calculations when using more than one font per line of text. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/incorrect-preferred-height-calculation-when-a-single-text-line-uses-different-font-assets" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104237</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Fixed incorrect preferred value calculations for text objects that contain characters and / or sprites whose character or glyph metrics have a scale = 0. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/contentsizefitter-calculates-incorrect-height-for-textmeshpro-text-containing-sprites-with-scale-factor-equals-0" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112064</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Fixed potential NRE when Multi Atlas Texture is used and text geometry exceeds 65535 vertices. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-is-thrown-when-setting-a-long-string-in-tmp-with-atlas-population-mode-set-to-dynamic-and-multi-atlas-textures-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120500</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Fixed preferred height calculations when text component is using multiple font asset with varying metrics. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-layout-system-is-failing-to-correctly-calculate-or-apply-the-height-of-the-japanese-fallback-font-when-the-primary-english-fonts-metrics-are-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122933</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Fixed preferred width calculations not taking into consideration kerning adjustments. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/an-extra-empty-line-appears-in-the-tmp-text-field-when-the-unity-ui-auto-layout-system-is-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120270</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Improve line breaking for Chinese and Japanese. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/line-break-position-changes-in-traditional-chinese-when-consecutive-punctuation-is-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120042</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Improved CJK line break handling. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/a-specific-project-freezes-when-content-size-fitters-horizontal-fit-is-set-to-min-size-and-it-contains-double-ellipses-followed-by-a-cjk-character" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121295</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: Revised the UI text component Maskable property to also control the RectMask2D clipping as well as ensuring its state is also mirrored on any potential sub mesh text objects. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-text-color-is-incorrect-when-text-is-rendered-on-submeshui" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119215</a>)

-   TextMeshPro: the Margins property now supports multi-object editing. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/margins-of-tmp-gameobjects-become-the-same-when-both-are-selected" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-91467</a>)

-   uGUI: Disabled culling of empty Rects in the Editor to avoid false positives in the Scene View. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-elements-are-not-visible-on-certain-x-axis-positions-when-the-rotation-of-the-y-axis-is-90-or-90-degrees" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-100206</a>)

-   uGUI: Ensure Pixel Perfect Rects are not rounded to zero width/height. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-image-disappears-at-certain-y-positions-when-the-game-view-resolution-is-smaller-than-the-canvas-reference-resolution" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103165</a>)

-   uGUI: Fixed a potential crash that occurred when a `CanvasRenderer` was destroyed while its parent canvas was nested under a disabled parent. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-ui-canvas-updateeventindexesrecursive-when-a-script-modifies-nested-canvases-in-play-mode-while-using-command-line-argument-debugallocator" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120152</a>)

-   uGUI: Fixed an editor bug where resizing the handle rect of a Slider and then performing an Undo causes its anchors to get reset. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/slider-handle-becomes-a-different-size-than-default-when-undoing-change-to-the-width-parameter" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108288</a>)

-   uGUI: Fixed an issue where the canvas scaler could invalidate the canvas more times than necessary. (UUM-114214)

-   uGUI: Fixed excessive rebuilding and resetting of Layout Groups. (UUM-120010)

-   uGUI: Fixed for flickering caret and highlight while selecting text in a TextMeshPro InputField while continuing to drag cursor above or below of the Input Field. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/textmeshpro-text-inputs-caret-is-flickering-when-line-mode-is-set-to-multi-line-and-text-is-selected" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122467</a>)

-   uGUI: Fixed Images being ignored by raycast when they were outside of a Masks bounds but had maskable set to false. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ugui-elements-behind-a-mask-are-not-clickable-when-maskable-is-disabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113314</a>)

-   uGUI: Fixed layout groups not updating when an empty RectTransform child changes size. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vertical-layout-group-doesnt-maintain-proper-spacing-when-nested-ui-elements-change-size-at-runtime" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103876</a>)

-   uGUI: Fixed layout groups not updating when an empty RectTransform child changes size. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vertical-layout-group-doesnt-maintain-proper-spacing-when-nested-ui-elements-change-size-at-runtime" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103876</a>)

-   uGUI: Nested canvases utilizing the Screen Space - Camera rendering mode will now fall back to sorting based on distance to their assigned camera. This fallback is used when Override Sorting is enabled, and only after first comparing sorting layer and sorting order values. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nested-canvases-with-the-same-order-in-layer-ignore-z-position-for-render-order-when-sharing-parent-canvas" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-100599</a>)

-   uGUI: Prevent users from calling Destroy or DestroyImmediate on the default uGUIMaterial to prevent a crash. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-unityengine-dot-canvas-getdefaultcanvasmaterial-when-loading-a-scene" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114080</a>)

-   uGUI: RawImage no longer crashes when attempting to utilize its assigned Texture when the value is an object reference of an invalid type. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-unityengine-dot-ui-dot-rawimage-onpopulatemesh-when-playing-raw-image-texture-animation-with-sprite-keyframes" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117371</a>)

-   uGUI: Revised an outdated editor warning that previously advised users to "clear the Packing tag." Since Packing tags are now obsolete, the message has been updated to reflect current best practices. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/warning-about-an-obsolete-feature-appears-when-image-type-is-set-to-tiled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103962</a>)

-   uGUI: Updated drag tracking to release all active drag events when the application loses focus. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/onenddrag-event-not-triggered-when-losing-editor-focus-during-simultaneous-left-and-right-mouse-drag" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104163</a>)

-   uGUI: Updated Image raycast behavior to ensure consistent handling of screenPoints outside bounds, preventing parent Images from blocking child Images. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/child-image-does-not-trigger-an-event-when-the-parent-image-is-using-alphahittestminimumthreshold-0" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110769</a>)

-   uGUI: Updated the text color in the Intercepted Events Preview to match the Editor style. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/intercepted-events-text-is-hardly-visible-in-inspector-window-for-ui-elements-when-editor-theme-is-set-to-light" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119688</a>)

-   UI Elements: Fixed event `BaseVerticalCollectionView.selectedIndicesChanged` to no longer reverse the order of selected indices during range selection that is moving to the *next* item (shift + key down on most systems). (RAT-2352)

-   UI Elements: Fixed the items not expanding on creation when autoExpand is set to true. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/multicolumntreeview-does-not-auto-expand-foldouts-when-autoexpand-is-set-to-true" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107968</a>)

-   UI Elements: Fixed the multiline TextField layout not stretching when flex-grow is applied to the Control. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-toolkits-textinput-element-does-not-inherit-layout-parameters-from-the-textfield-element-when-textfield-dot-multiline-is-true" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122632</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Added additional checks to the TreeView control prevent the API from breaking when misused. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/treeview-dot-additem-performance-regression-when-opening-editorwindow" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112202</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Added clarity around selection in the samples window. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unexpected-behaviour-when-selecting-items-in-ui-toolkits-treeview-sample" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-87336</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Added missing converter between a RenderTexture and a StyleBackground. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unable-to-bind-rendertexture-to-the-visualelements-stylebackground-property" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120865</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Added missing UxmlAttribute converters for byte, sbyte, short and ushort.
