-   UI Toolkit: Added support for Foldouts to toggle when the UI Builder inspector is in readonly mode. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/some-of-the-ui-builder-disabled-element-dropdowns-cannot-be-opened" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112929</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Added UI Document and StyleSheet file types in create menu entries. (UUM-116809)

-   UI Toolkit: Added UsageHints.LargePixelCoverage, which can be used on a large VisualElement (e.g. panel background) to force the use of a specialized shader that is more GPU friendly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-uitoolkit-fps-drops-slash-increase-in-time-consumption-in-the-ui-toolkit-rendering-when-adding-style-dot-backgroundcolor-and-slash-or-style-dot-backgroundimage-to-the-visualelement" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-91516</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Added `ClearValue` API to allow any numeric input field to be cleared. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/uitk-controls-non-text-inputfield-cant-be-reset-by-api" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122277</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Automatically set the alpha channel of unset style color properties to 1 when picked through the color picker. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/in-ui-builder-background-colours-start-with-an-alpha-of-0-which-can-be-easily-missed-by-users" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-76955</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Changed Slider, SliderInt and MinMaxSlider low, high and value attributes to be delayed in the UI Builder inspector. This allows for typing the full value without the clamping being applied whilst you are still typing. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/slider-rounding-does-not-allow-setting-certain-valid-numbers-as-values-when-low-and-high-values-not-set-to-default" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-90139</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Clicks in dead zones of the canvas selection header won't select the element underneath in UI Builder. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builder-viewports-tool-gizmo-has-deadzones" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107380</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Correctly load asset if type is already known. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/svgs-with-a-gradient-are-displayed-incorrectly-in-the-ui-toolkit-when-loaded-from-the-resource-folder" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110641</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Correctly support multi-threading in UIToolkit worldspace rendering. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-tlsf-free-when-rendering-ui-toolkit-panel-in-world-space-on-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109817</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Custom material properties can now be set in USS stylesheets. (UUM-116988)

-   UI Toolkit: Do not start drag and drop if the target element gets hidden after the pointer down event. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/list-item-is-re-selected-when-moving-the-mouse-after-clearselection-function" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113109</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fix the stylesheet button in the UI Debugger appearing to do something when no source is available for the stylesheet (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editor-default-stylesheet-slash-matching-selector-buttons-in-debugger-dont-do-anything" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-87670</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed "Extract inline style" option bug in UIBuilder. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builder-doesnt-update-the-style-sheet-when-using-extract-inline-style-right-click-option" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105327</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed a cosmetic issue where toolbar buttons shrank when the container shrank. (UUM-104876)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed a problem when measuring the text size using ATG with an explicit font size on a textElement. (UUM-116728)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed a `NullReferenceException` that occurred when the UI Toolkit Debugger was in an inactive tab. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/error-nullreferenceexception-object-reference-not-set-to-an-instance-of-an-object-is-present-when-the-ui-toolkit-debugger-window-is-docked-and-inactive-on-editor-launch" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117267</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed all UnityEvent listeners that are inherited class members are unassigned (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/persisted-event-listeners-are-reset-when-collapsing-and-expanding-a-component-in-the-inspector" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111210</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an error that occurred when reading a StyleSheet keyword value from a property name. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/errors-are-thrown-and-functionality-breaks-when-using-a-uss-class-with-transition-property-none-in-ui-builder" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116350</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an exception thrown by the runtime dropdown field when choices list is empty. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/argumentoutofrangeexception-is-thrown-when-an-empty-dropdownfield-is-clicked-at-runtime" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105552</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an exception when restoring the UI Debugger context (UUM-114690)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an exception when trying to convert a list of null assets to a UXML list. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-and-list-items-not-saved-when-adding-elements-to-list-uxmlattribute-in-ui-builder" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116892</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an exceptions when displaying PropertyFields bound to an array named "Array" in the inspector. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/binding-listview-failed-warning-is-thrown-when-a-gameobject-with-an-attached-script-containing-an-array-or-list-named-array-is-selected" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110573</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an intermittent crash on AppleTV that occurred during the `Painter2D.ClosePath()` call. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/tvos-exc-bad-access-error-is-thrown-when-painter2d-dot-closepath-is-called" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105548</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue in the UI Builder where UXML attributes representing serialize references were not overridden correctly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builder-does-not-detect-changes-to-serializereference-fields-when-modifying-custom-uxml-control-attributes" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112944</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue on macOS where right-clicking on a TextField did not select the text before opening the context menu. (UUM-102230)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue so pressing the *Fit Viewport* button now centres a canvas in the UI Builder if the canvas size is 0,0. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/fit-viewport-doesnt-work-if-uxml-document-output-is-resized-to-the-smallest-possible-size" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109243</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue so that multiple `PanelInputConfiguration` components now wait until entering Play mode to become disabled. (UUM-108599)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where binding to a GradientField or a CurveField could hang the editor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editor-freezes-when-changing-gradient-or-animationcurve-fields-with-the-runtime-binding-system" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103737</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where C# keywords could not be used as identifiers when code-generating the property bags. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/source-generation-with-generatepropertybag-fails-when-using-reserved-keywords-as-variable-names-with-at-prefix" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113921</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where debug names would not appear when pressing the Alt key in debug inspector mode. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/uitoolkit-inspector-property-names-dont-show-up-correctly-when-inspector-is-in-debug-mode" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-18712</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where nullable references could break compilation if they appeared before the type definition. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/generatepropertybag-code-generation-fails-when-nullable-is-used-with-the-partial-class-modifier" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115834</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where the IMGUI TreeView's context menu option to rename wouldn't work when the containing view was not focused. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/gameobject-cannot-be-renamed-when-right-clicked-and-a-different-window-is-in-focus" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110067</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where the mouse became permanently unresponsive on other world space documents after destroying a world space document while it was being clicked. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-toolkit-input-becomes-unresponsive-when-the-uidocument-gameobject-is-destroyed" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117081</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where the scene was always made dirty when inspecting UI Documents. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-docment-marked-dirty-when-inspected" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115255</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue where UI Documents appeared in the wrong sort order when entering Play mode. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/uidocument-sort-order-is-randomly-wrong" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119306</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue with PropertyFields bound to an array named "Array" throwing an exception when the add button was clicked. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/nullreferenceexception-error-is-thrown-when-attempting-to-add-an-element-to-an-array-or-list-named-array-in-the-inspector" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110572</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed an issue with the TreeView where releasing the pointer during a Drag And Drop operation raised an exception. (UUM-66397)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed ArgumentException when scheduling action during DetachFromPanelEvent and then moving the element to another active panel (UUM-117338)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed Bindings field completer popup not closing in the UI Builder when the field loses focus. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-data-source-path-dropdown-in-the-edit-binding-does-not-get-hidden-correctly" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-47794</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed bound fields not showing an indication if they were animated or driven. (UUM-105089)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed change events being sent when the text had not changed, but the precision of the text value did not exactly match the float value. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-inspector-rounds-up-float-values-up-to-a-maximum-of-7-digits-and-overwrites-the-actual-values-in-the-file-when-saving-the-asset" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-100527</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed cosmetic issue with the padding of sections in the UI Builder inspector. (UUM-86658)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed crash when stroking rounded joins with Painter2D. -   UI Toolkit: Fixed dirty builder state on switching to a document with a different canvas size. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/switching-between-ui-documents-with-different-canvas-sizes-marks-the-uxml-file-as-dirty" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107410</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed dirty builder state on UXML and USS changes. (UUM-105717)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed disabling and reenabling UI Document leaving extra GameObjects under the EventSystem. -   UI Toolkit: Fixed elements sometimes still referenced in memory after being removed from their panel. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/memory-address-leaks-when-using-visual-elements-in-a-specific-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109369</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed event debugger event group now toggles each event checkbox immediately. (UUM-104871)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed excessive empty space is present when observing Editor tabs. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/excessive-empty-space-is-present-when-observing-editor-tabs" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108287</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed flickering of the data source and path view when refreshing the UIBuilder inspector. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/inspector-window-flickers-when-a-selector-is-created-using-a-style-class-list-section" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-97556</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed focus in UI Builder code preview when clicking on ScrollView controls. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builder-scroll-button-for-code-preview-section-automatically-selects-all-of-the-code" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105775</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed generated UxmlElement and UxmlObject code not fully qualifying some types. -   UI Toolkit: Fixed gradient fields in UI Builder not using correct timing numbers, causing a green key to appear out of place. -   UI Toolkit: Fixed help link for the PanelTextSetting asset. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/missing-page-when-opening-reference-for-panel-text-settings" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-92901</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed highlight color of the uxml header when doing a drag and drop from the Library. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/dragging-any-ui-element-under-the-uxml-file-in-the-ui-builder-hierarchy-doesnt-highlight-it" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-76953</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed hover state update on world-space documents when the mouse isn't moving. (UUM-108595)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed ImmediateModeElement not clipping and drawing with an invalid offset in the Game View. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/offset-rendering-occurs-when-drawing-a-custom-immediatemodeelement" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110223</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed inconsistent capitalisation of the word Binding in UI Builder. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/inconsistent-capitalisation-of-the-word-binding" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110771</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed lack of cohesion and spacing in UI Builder's variables section. (UUM-97031)

(<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builder-value-is-not-selected-when-left-clicking-on-spacing-slash-border-widget-values" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105818</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed: Ensure margin gizmo stays visible while dragging. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builder-viewports-gizmos-for-margin-and-padding-disappear-when-dragging-to-modify-the-value-and-the-cursor-leaves-the-spacing-section" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105900</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed: No exceptions when opening a file and renaming its first element. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/notsupportedexception-error-is-thrown-when-renaming-components-in-ui-builder" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107900</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed: Prevented resizing and moving a VisualElement in the UI Builder that is scaled or rotated, this is not supported. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builders-resize-guide-line-changes-position-and-doubles-when-dragging-the-edges-of-an-angled-visual-element" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107640</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed: Preview tab not expanding correctly in certain editor windows. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/preview-tab-in-the-select-texture-2d-window-does-not-expand-when-the-window-is-minimized" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107475</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed: Selected Style Fields in the UI Builder now have their label highlighted. (UUM-107584)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed: Text in the new selector field in UI Builder is no longer covered by the pseudo states button. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/add-new-selector-input-text-is-partially-covered-by-the-add-pseudo-class-button" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109121</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed: Using an Editor-only element in a UIDocument now triggers a warning in the Console and displays an error in the Inspector window of the UIDocument. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/adding-color-element-in-ui-builder-breaks-color-picker" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105809</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixed: \[UI Builder\] Fixed an issue where VisualElements could not be renamed when clicking on another VisualElement. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/name-of-the-first-renamed-visual-element-does-not-change-in-the-ui-builder-hierarchy-when-its-being-renamed-in-the-inspector" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109114</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Fixes an issue where a null value of type object would not be convertible to any other type. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-binding-event-is-not-triggered-when-the-object-of-custom-type-is-set-to-null" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112074</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Improved memory layout of UxmlSerializedData to reduce padding. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/improve-uxmlserializeddata-generation-to-reduce-padding" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108363</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Improved search result message in UI Toolkit Layout Debugger. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/last-item-reached-warning-is-thrown-when-no-search-results-are-found-in-ui-toolkit-layout-debugger" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115950</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Made UI Toolkit use the advanced text generator by default.

-   UI Toolkit: Prevent the ScrollView from scrolling when the scrollbar is visible and the content does not overflow. (UUM-111723)

-   UI Toolkit: Prevented a KeyNotFoundException in UI Toolkit's Layout Debugger when clicking \<Layout Loop++>. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/keynotfoundexception-error-thrown-when-pressing-layout-loop-plus-plus-in-ui-toolkit-layout-debugger-button" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-101747</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Prevented a NullReferenceException in UI Toolkit's Layout Debugger when clicking \<Goto next complex update>. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/pressing-goto-next-complex-update-in-ui-toolkit-layout-debugger-throws-nullreferenceexception-error" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-101739</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: RadioButtonGroup loses selection when reattached to Hierarchy (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/radiobuttongroup-loses-selection-when-reattached-to-hierarchy" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114566</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: Removed extraneous shaders from UI Document references. (UUM-120303)

-   UI Toolkit: Removed unused background repeat fields in canvas settings in UI Builder. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/non-editable-repeat-x-and-repeat-y-settings-appear-when-ui-builder-canvas-background-is-set-to-image" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122232</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: ScrollView.ScrollTo will now defer requests when the layout is dirty. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/scrollview-dot-scrollto-method-does-not-work-when-trying-to-scroll-to-an-item-that-has-been-added-in-the-same-frame" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-101776</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: The Remove button in a `ListView` footer will be disabled when list is empty. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/tags-and-layers-window-has-inconsistent-remove-button-behaviour-between-tags-sorting-layers-and-rendering-layers-when-there-are-no-removable-items-in-the-list" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122811</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: The UI Builder now allows for wider selection of elements in the canvas. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/ui-builder-inconsistent-selection-when-element-is-outside-of-canvas-bounds" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-74631</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: The UXML file importer will now show a button to fix issues with Template and Style src paths that have changed but can still be resolved via their guid. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/warning-prompt-with-apply-overwrite-files-button-is-not-available-when-associated-template-is-moved" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116534</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: UI Builder tooltip values in the inspector have a maximum number of characters. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/maskfield-choices-elements-tooltip-covers-the-entire-window-while-scrolling-through-the-elements" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122152</a>)

-   UI Toolkit: UIDocument sortingOrder property is now ignored and removed from the UIDocument inspector when rendering in world space. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/sort-order-should-not-appear-in-root-uidocument-when-in-a-world-space-panel" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117484</a>)

-   Undo System: Changes to objects which do not change the scene no longer trigger a scene dirty. (UUM-114657)

-   Undo System: Fixed issue with recording actions into the undo system. (UUM-120641)

-   Undo System: Fixed to block all calls to Undo during Asset Import. (UUM-100918)

-   Undo System: Keep undo stack in sync during project load by pausing serialization. (UUM-97070)

-   Undo System: Prevent adding more modifications while generating of undo actions. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editor-crashes-on-typetreequeries-isstreamedbinarycompatible-or-typetreeiterator-getnode-when-entering-the-play-mode-in-the-editor" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110255</a>)

-   UNET: Fixed the UI Builder's selector pill being frozen when dragging and scrolling at the same time. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/selector-gets-stuck-in-ui-if-scroll-is-used-while-dragging" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-93877</a>)

-   Universal RP: Added conditional shader renaming for Autodesk Interactive shaders to MaterialUpgrader. (UUM-113600)

-   Universal RP: Added missing apv keywords to TerrainLitAdd.shader. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/distant-shadowmask-does-not-cast-shadows-on-terrain-when-baking-with-adaptive-probe-volumes" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-100526</a>)

-   Universal RP: Depth Priming requires a full prepass to the activeCameraDepth. In deferred, a partial prepass should not be considered depth priming. (UUM-122756)

-   Universal RP: Fix errors & black screen when resetting active URP Asset from UI context menu (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/resetting-pc-rp-asset-universal-render-pipeline-asset-causes-game-view-to-become-black-and-throws-renderer-and-nullreference-errors-in-the-console-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114211</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed ''Depth Texture Mode' on the URP Renderer should only have effect when the 'Depth Texture' is checked on the URP asset or Camera. Selecting 'Force Prepass' now does not add the pass when 'Depth Texture' is off. (UUM-120047)

-   Universal RP: Fixed a missing intermediate color texture in the `ColorCopy` pass when rendering to a `RenderTexture` asset (offscreen rendering). (UUM-119604)

-   Universal RP: Fixed an issue where a main light was disabled in the URP Asset. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/lights-are-being-culled-and-appear-pixelated-when-the-main-light-property-is-disabled-and-forward-plus-or-deferred-plus-rendering-paths-are-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104391</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed and issue where SSAO was rendered inverted when the AfterOpaque setting is used without intermediate attachments allocated by the camera. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urp-in-the-player-ssao-is-flipped-when-after-opaque-is-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-73078</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed black screen when using lens flare with bloom mip bias 0. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/game-view-fails-to-render-and-becomes-dark-when-the-screen-space-lens-flare-components-intensity-is-set-to-1-and-the-bloom-mip-bias-is-set-to-0" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109463</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed bloom and screen space lens flare on tiny resolutions by fixing use of incorrect bloom mip pyramid texture. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/scene-views-breaks-and-errors-are-spammed-in-the-console-when-the-game-view-is-shrunk-vertically-to-the-minimum-and-the-aspect-ratio-is-16-9-or-16-10" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108649</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed incorrect input color in the user LUT sampling when HDR output enabled. (UUM-90785)

-   Universal RP: Fixed missing asset icon for URP Asset after creating it while typing its name. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urp-asset-has-no-icon-when-creating-a-new-urp-asset-and-is-not-selected-after-creation" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114896</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed missing shader variant in URP when drawing to an offscreen depth only image. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-lights-dont-exhibit-shadows-when-strict-shader-variant-matching-is-disabled-in-windows-standalone-player" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105409</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed Render Graph failing to handle Gizmos rendering with a DepthCopy pass when using SubmitRenderRequest in URP. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/attempting-to-render-to-a-depth-only-surface-with-no-dummy-color-attachment-warning-is-thrown-when-calling-submitrenderrequest-in-urp-from-begincamerarendering-with-gizmos-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-91335</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed SSAO precision issue on Mobile devices. (UUM-119495)

-   Universal RP: Fixed sticky m_RenderStateBlock.depthState in the Forward Opaque (Only) pass. The state wasn't cleared to the default after depth priming was turned on and off again. In this case, ZWrite was forced on, ignoring ZWrite properties in the shader. (UUM-117359)

-   Universal RP: Fixed the issue where Mismatch fragment errors are thrown when STP is enabled and Render Object Pass set to AfterRenderingPostProcessing. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/multiple-errors-occur-when-setting-the-render-object-event-to-afterrenderingpostprocessing-while-using-stp-for-upscaling" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111329</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed URPPreprocessBuild.OnPostProcessBuild not getting called. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urppreprocessbuild-dot-onpostprocessbuild-is-never-executed-when-building-the-player" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104655</a>)

-   Universal RP: Fixed Warning logged when injecting a pass on AfterRenderPostProcessing + X (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/warnings-are-logged-and-rendererfeature-that-requests-an-intermediate-texture-does-not-work-when-adding-an-offset-to-the-renderpassevent" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109361</a>)

-   Universal RP: Updated UI for Screen Space Shadows scriptable renderer feature.

-   URP: Added check logic related to OpenGL devices in DecalRenderFeature. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/decal-technique-set-to-automatic-prefers-the-dbuffer-option-when-using-opengl" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107966</a>)

-   URP: Fixed an issue in UberPostProcessPass where invalid texture handles could be reused across execution passes, leading to a Render Graph execution error. (UUM-109708)

-   URP: Fixed an issue to avoid persistent memory allocation (`_CameraTargetAttachmentX` and `_CameraUpscaledTargetAttachmentX`) in RenderGraph when possible (single camera), and use a RenderGraph texture instead. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/cameratargetattachmentb-unnecessarily-allocates-memory-even-when-post-processing-is-disabled-and-camera-stacking-is-not-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99384</a>)

-   URP: Fixed an issue where nothing was rendered when using `Graphics.DrawProceduralIndirectNow` in the `OnRenderObject` method. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/apple-m-chip-android-ios-nothing-is-rendered-in-the-game-view-slash-application-when-using-graphics-dot-drawproceduralindirectnow-with-urp-specified-shader" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-96000</a>)

-   URP: Fixed an issue where RenderingLayersTexture was not correctly registered in the ScreenSpaceDecal pass, resulting in missing or incorrect entries in the Render Graph Viewer. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/certain-textures-are-incorrectly-marked-in-the-render-pass-list-when-observed-through-render-graph-viewer" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109755</a>)

-   URP: Fixed an issue where the Render Pipeline (RP) Converter did not display the results of the converter's execution in the Console window. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urprenderpipelineconverter-no-message-in-the-console-after-the-successful-slash-failed-conversion" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-97117</a>)

-   URP: Fixed depth backbuffer support with Gizmos in Game view, effectively removing the incorrect warning message "CommandBuffer: built-in render texture type 3 not found while executing.". (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/gizmos-are-not-rendering-in-game-view" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104383</a>)

-   URP: Fixed IndexOutOfRangeException by allocating size with respect to visible light's length. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/an-unclear-error-occurs-when-exceeding-the-light-limit-set-in-urp-config-package" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102022</a>)

-   URP: Fixed issue to now perform check that GBuffers are valid before using them in the DecalGBufferRenderPass. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/game-view-turns-black-scene-view-turns-grey-console-error-nullreferenceexception-when-wireframe-is-turned-on-because-the-decalgbufferrenderpass-references-a-non-existent-gbuffer" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103836</a>)

-   URP: Fixed LensFlareCommonSRP in OpenGL ES ignoring objects between directional light and camera. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/lens-flare-renders-without-occlusion-when-the-light-source-is-obstructed-by-solid-objects-when-running-on-android-using-opengl" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110294</a>)

-   URP: Fixed screen space shadows artifacts when using Vulkan on some Android devices (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vulkan-artifacts-appear-in-the-player-when-using-screen-space-shadows-with-orthographic-camera" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116048</a>)

-   URP: Fixed spotlights with small angles not being rendered on mobile devices in URP (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/spot-light-is-not-rendered-if-the-outer-spot-angle-is-set-to-below-2-dot-56216-when-the-android-build-platform-is-selected" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104997</a>)

-   URP: Fixed SSAO depth issue on Mali GPU (Android) devices. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-artifacts-are-present-when-using-specific-material-and-shader-on-devices-with-exynos" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-75485</a>)

-   URP: Fixed SSAOOnly pass in the deferred path to be compatible with Native Render Pass. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/screen-space-ambient-occlusion-is-not-visible-when-the-directional-light-intensity-is-0-dot-001-or-lower-with-the-deferred-rendering-path-selected" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113638</a>)

-   URP: Fixed unclear warnings when user creates more lights than the light limit when using Deferred rendering path. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/property-additionalshadowparams-exceeds-previous-array-size-41-vs-32-dot-dot-dot-warning-is-spammed-when-exceeding-the-light-limit-set-in-urp-config-package" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102023</a>)

-   URP: Save URP asset before starting an asset bundle build. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/urp-is-using-in-memory-assets-without-saving-them" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-126809</a>)

-   URP: Updated Forward+ and Deferred+ rendering so that reflection probes with a null texture are no longer included in clustering. Previously, this caused the clustering code to use outdated reflection probe data after the texture was removed, which could occur when clearing baked reflection probe data while using these renderers. (UUM-116088)

-   Version Control: Added Beyond Compare 5 support to Yaml Merge. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/beyond-compare-5-is-not-supported-by-yaml-merge" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109861</a>)

-   Version Control: Fixed a NullReferenceException that was raised on double-clicking an asset that is "Copied (new)" (new file added from a shelve).

-   Version Control: Fixed a NullReferenceException that was raised when the workspace is removed with Pending Changes.

-   Version Control: Fixed an error that was thrown when selecting *Configure Unity Version Control* in the Build Automation settings in Build Profiles. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/failed-to-open-uvcs-window-error-is-thrown-when-the-configure-unity-version-control-button-is-pressed-in-the-build-automation-settings-in-build-profiles" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115391</a>)

-   Version Control: Fixed applying shelves when some files cannot be checked out due to exclusive checkout lock rules. These files are now applied as local changes instead of failing the whole operation.

-   Version Control: Fixed AssetImportWorkers crash on compiling ASMDEF while collab-proxy package is upgrading. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/assetimportworkers-crash-when-asmdef-are-deleted" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-107732</a>)

-   Version Control: Fixed double indentation in the Project Browser when Version Control is enabled. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/extra-spacing-in-the-project-tab-appears-when-using-perforce" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112555</a>)

-   Version Control: Fixed incorrect tab name showing as "Unity.PlasticSCM.Editor.PlasticW" with no icon when upgrading a project to Unity 6.x.

-   Version Control: Fixed Inspector and Project's Asset Menu to allow to Check in "Changed" items.

-   Version Control: Fixed Inspector and Project's Asset Menu to allow to Undo "Changed" items.

-   Version Control: Fixed issue in UnityYAMLMerge not able to read files under multibyte character paths. (UUM-119455)

-   Version Control: Fixed Library folder that was sometimes wrongly added to source control on a new project created from the Hub.

-   Version Control: Fixed the "unknown" status displayed after restoring a workspace that was previously removed.

-   Version Control: Fixed the alignment of Unity Version Control's settings with the title of the window. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/version-control-information-does-not-align-with-window-title" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122499</a>)

-   Version Control: Fixed the Changesets view that could crash the Editor when displaying a very long changeset comment, with "PPtr\<Shader>::operator Shader\*() const".

-   Version Control: Fixed the console error that occured when using CTRL-H keyboard shortcut to Hide a branch.

-   Version Control: Fixed the Controlled and Private overlay icons reverted back to earlier versions, based on user feedback.

-   Version Control: Fixed the diff panel keyboard shortcuts that didn't take into account the focus to open the diff on the correct item.

-   Version Control: Fixed the GUILayout error that occured when applying a shelve with pending merge link.

-   Version Control: Fixed the Item checkbox that was unresponsive when the Pending Changes list is empty. It is now disabled.

-   Version Control: Fixed the merge error message that didn't fit in available space and was truncated.

-   Version Control: Fixed the merge link text that was not centered vertically with its icon.

-   Version Control: Fixed the missing "Hide" contextual menu on the Unity Version Control button in the Unity 6.3 Toolbar.

-   Version Control: Fixed the new "Create Branch based on" dialog variant that was truncated, hiding the buttons, in 2.10.0.

-   Version Control: Fixed the pending changes success message appearing in the status bar instead of the main area when this one is empty.

-   Version Control: Fixed the Pending changes view that was refreshed on window activation for no reason.

-   Version Control: Fixed the Shelve view that was missing a keyboard shortcut to open the diff in the Desktop Application.

-   Version Control: Fixed the Toolbar button icon that was not visible for uncontrolled projects for Editor older than Unity 6.3.

-   Version Control: Fixed the Toolbar button showing an "unknown" status after removal of the workspace.

-   Version Control: Fixed the Toolbar button that should be visible by default in 6.3+.

-   Version Control: Fixed the Undo changes operation so that it can now apply to the selection even if it contains some Private files.

-   Version Control: UnityYAMLMerge tool was adding long path specifiers to the file paths passed to the fallback merge tools, causing p4Merge to fail. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/unityyamlmerge-fails-to-merge-on-some-computers-when-some-specific-files-are-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-126068</a>)

-   Version Control: Update PerforcePlugin to use ARM64 arch for ARM64 MacEditor - P4 API 2023.1 or higher (UUM-43482)

-   Version Control: Updated the Perforce client with P4 API 2021.2/2201121 to support Perforce P4 Cloud (Helix Core Cloud). (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/perforce-client-does-not-support-p4-helix-core-cloud-upgrade-to-version-2021-dot-2-slash-2201121-or-later" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113016</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Add icons on VFXRenderer. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-renderer-properties-in-animation-window-doesnt-use-any-icons" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114864</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Added a context menu option to insert a template at the mouse position. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/newly-created-vfx-graph-template-appears-in-the-same-of-the-graph-view-space-even-when-the-space-is-not-empty" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102573</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Added clear in graph error feedback when a strip child system capacity does not match its parent system. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-graph-capacity-issue-when-using-gpuevent-and-strips" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-72015</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Adjust "Prewarm Time" input field to fit 2 digits of precision. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/numeric-input-field-for-prewarm-time-doesnt-fully-display-decimal-values" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113612</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Adjust padding on Sub Mesh Mask label for mesh outputs. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/sub-mesh-mask-field-in-vfx-graph-has-no-padding" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113945</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Avoid text cutoff when a context name is really long. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/node-name-gets-cut-off-in-the-visual-effect-graph-when-the-name-is-long" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114091</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Clarify documentation about "Enable Ray Tracing" setting. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/lack-of-documentation-regarding-vfx-ray-tracing-support" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103422</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fix CurrentThread::IsMainThread() assertion being fired in Legacy Graphics Jobs mode (UUM-107647)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed "An edge with the same input and output already exists" error popping in the graph. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/an-edge-with-the-same-input-and-output-already-exists-is-thrown-when-operators-are-connected-to-blocks" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120233</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed "Transform has 'VFXManager' change interests present..." errors. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/transform-has-vfxmanager-change-interests-present-dot-dot-dot-when-changing-scenes" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115873</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed a bug with in graph compilation error/warning badges not being displayed.

-   VFX Graph: Fixed a memory leak that occurred when using motion vectors. (UUM-122249)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed a wrapping issue in the VFX Graph Control Panel. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/custom-particle-system-names-textboxes-are-not-correctly-wrapped-in-the-vfx-control-panel" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114087</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an error message when closing the editor with the VFX Graph node search popup opened on macOS. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/create-node-popup-remains-and-a-nullreferenceexception-error-is-thrown-when-the-create-node-popup-is-opened-and-the-project-is-reopened" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114024</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an exception in Template Window when selecting a category and clicking on the create button. (UUM-103882)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an exception that could happen when double clicking on a separator in the node search. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/clicking-on-section-text-in-node-library-context-folder-throws-nullreferenceexception-errors" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105303</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an exception that occurred with the Inspector when a block exposed a skinned mesh renderer. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/exposing-skinned-mesh-property-in-subgraph-throws-errors-when-interacting-with-the-parent-graph" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102357</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an issue in Shader Graph where Parallax Occlusion Mapping nodes were ignored after compilation. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/parallax-occlusion-mapping-shadergraph-node-does-not-work-correctly-with-vfx-graph" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110541</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an issue in the Visual Effect Graph where the window disappeared after using the Save As option with the same asset name. The window now remains open in this scenario. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/saving-visual-effect-graph-closes-it-in-visual-effect-graph-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121821</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an issue where an exposed property value wasn't properly restored with multi-selection. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/property-values-are-not-restored-for-all-objects-when-re-enabled-after-reset" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113957</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an issue where duplicated properties in the blackboard were not ordered correctly. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/duplicating-the-properties-in-the-vfx-graph-blackboard-pastes-them-on-the-top-of-the-list-instead-below-the-original-property" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122054</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an issue where the Graph Debug Information window did not display information when a GameObject was attached. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-graph-debug-information-window-does-not-display-information-and-shows-select-a-gameobject-running-this-vfx-warning-when-the-gameobject-is-already-selected" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113047</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an issue where the preview was too bright in HDRP lit scenarios. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-appears-white-and-lacks-color-in-preview-window-when-created-and-observed-in-hdrp-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119331</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed an issue with custom hlsl when line comments are put on top of the code for an operator. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-graph-compilation-fails-when-custom-hlsl-starts-with-multiple-slash-slash-comment-lines" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116688</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed blackboard losing selection when scrolling. (UUM-103964)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed capitalization issue and also white space was not preserved. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/inconsistent-node-search-results-in-vfx-graph" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-122516</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed crash on some platforms when From and To Vector3 values in the Look At node are equal. (UUM-120949)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed enum field was not set properly and then UI was always resetting to default value. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-graph-subgraph-enum-slot-values-are-reset-upon-ui-refresh" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109559</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed errors that occurred when undoing the action of moving a property into the output category in the Blackboard. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/subgraph-operator-dragging-property-to-output-category-and-undoing-throws-console-errors" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103967</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed inconsistency with a wrap mode "hold". (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/visual-effect-control-track-continues-looping-when-wrap-mode-is-set-to-hold" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-99451</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed issue so now you can forbid dragging a subgraph block onto the graph directly, only allow on compatible contexts. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/subgraph-block-cannot-be-dragged-and-dropped-to-the-workspace-and-it-throws-errors" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104079</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed issue to now show "radiusScale" property when "Frustum culling" option is enabled on Shader Graph mesh outputs. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-radius-scale-option-is-removed-from-the-visual-effect-graph-output-particle-when-lod-is-disabled-in-particles-options" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108679</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed issue to now use display name instead of reference name for shaderKeyword integration with Shader Graph. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-graph-exposed-sg-keyword-property-display-their-ref-name-in-uppercase" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103871</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed node search window was not compliant with light theme colors. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/node-library-window-sections-text-in-context-folder-are-not-visible-when-editor-is-set-to-light-theme" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105299</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed sticky note text change size when edited if the size is set to medium, lard or huge. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-graph-sticky-note-font-size-changes-when-editing-text" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-92254</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed system turning invalid after disconnect/connect when mixing particle system outputs with particle strip data. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/mixing-strip-and-regular-contexts-prevents-vfx-from-rendering" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115053</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed the output port order in `CustomHLSL` operators. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/return-value-and-out-parameter-get-scrambled-when-a-custom-hlsl-operator-is-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121354</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed to avoid an exception when creating a new VFX asset from a template using the + button. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/trying-to-insert-template-to-subgraph-operator-or-block-throws-nullreferenceexception-error" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111630</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed: Avoid DPI warning message in the console. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/trying-to-access-the-dpi-setting-of-a-visual-element-that-is-not-on-a-panel-warnings-are-thrown-when-changing-the-shape-of-a-vfx-changing-it-back-and-saving-the-asset" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108361</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed: Non exposed properties aren't using default value. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-graph-unexposed-sg-property-default-value-are-not-fetch-by-vfx-graph" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103873</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed: Refresh Gizmo on Model Change to handle shape switches. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-graph-set-position-shape-gizmo-isnt-refreshed-after-shaper-switch" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104548</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed: Six-way lighting : fix APV indirect lighting when the VFX system is in world space. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/hdrp-6-way-vfx-shader-is-lit-incorrectly-when-world-coordinates-are-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108240</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Fixed: When the word template is used as a Unity feature then capitalize the word. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-word-template-is-not-capitalised-consistently-throughout-the-insert-a-template-into-current-vfx-asset-window" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102556</a>)

-   VFX Graph: GPU Time Threshold in Heatmap Parameters clamped to positive values only. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/gpu-time-threshold-in-vfx-graph-heatmap-parameters-can-be-set-to-a-negative-value" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114294</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Handling correctly ShaderGraph where only one SRP support VFX but not the current one. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-is-not-being-compiled-when-unticking-support-vfx-graph-setting-when-both-urp-and-hdrp-are-used-as-active-targets" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115004</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Limit the maximum number of characters to 128 for a group title to avoid any visual glitch. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-creating-a-long-group-node-name-breaks-nodes-boarders" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111257</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Limit Visual Effect asset prewarm step count to a max of 2400 steps. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/changing-prewarm-parameters-on-vfx-graph-leads-to-significant-performance-drops-and-freeze" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-119454</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Make the expand/collapse behavior and UI more consistent with the editor. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-graph-properties-transform-has-a-click-animation-when-clicked-on-the-transform-text-but-the-drop-down-does-not-open" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120164</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Missing dirty while modifying ShaderGraph Output. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/changes-in-output-inspector-surface-options-do-not-mark-the-asset-as-dirty-and-after-undoing-edits-to-particle-options-values-the-graph-is-marked-as-dirty" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-115015</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Opening Twice the same subgraph was possible after domain reload. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-same-subgraph-window-can-be-opened-multiple-times-after-a-domain-reload" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113965</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Prevent the output category in the blackboard from being duplicatable, renamable or deletable. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/subgraph-operator-rename-delete-duplicate-operations-are-present-and-functional-in-context-menu-of-output-category" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-103966</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Prevented multiple outputs in the same graph from having the same shader name. (UUM-117582)

-   VFX Graph: Properly handle empty enum values in the blackboard and now forbid to remove the last enum value so the list is never empty. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/removing-all-enum-values-from-uint-property-in-vfx-blackboard-causes-argumentoutofrangeexception-error-and-breaks-blackboard" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-120288</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Properly support \\r special character in custom hlsl tooltip comments (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-graph-hlsl-tooltips-issue-with-invible-characer" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109392</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Refresh attached VisualEffect when visual effect component changes. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/the-attach-button-is-active-and-shows-the-attached-to-visual-effect-tooltip-when-the-visual-effect-graph-is-no-longer-attached" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113966</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Restored panel visibility when opening the VFX Graph Editor and ensured that panels remain visible when entering Play mode. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/some-vfx-window-panels-close-on-entering-play-mode" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114102</a>)

-   VFX Graph: The title of a group could not be edited anymore when the title has been emptied. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vfx-deleting-new-group-node-name-doesnt-allow-to-type-or-add-new-name" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111258</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Turn context's display name label back to default text color in the inspector for better readability in light theme. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/usability-context-name-is-difficult-to-read-in-inspector-when-editor-theme-is-set-to-light" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110231</a>)

-   VFX Graph: Usability fixes when setting prewarm values in visual effect asset inspector (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/setting-the-the-prewarm-delta-time-to-the-smallest-value-always-sets-the-prewarm-step-count-to-the-different-random-values" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114276</a>)

-   Video: Fix freezing video playback on Galaxy S25 and similar Android devices (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/videoplayer-freezes-or-stops-on-certain-android-devices-when-enabling-and-disabling-the-video-multiple-times" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111599</a>)

-   Video: Fixed an issue where the VideoPlayer component failed to play MP4 files from StreamingAssets on some Android devices using Vulkan. The required NV12 decode shaders are now correctly included, restoring video playback on affected hardware. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/vulkan-on-certain-android-devices-unitys-videoplayer-component-fails-to-play-mp4-files-located-under-the-streamingassets-folder" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105019</a>)

-   Video: Fixed crash on older GPUs that don't support ID3D11Fence. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/crash-on-verifyd3dtexturesharingcallback-when-creating-a-new-project" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104855</a>)

-   Video: Fixed inconsistent imports of video files on MacOS. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/new-artifact-generated-for-video-when-reimporting-with-identical-settings" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104688</a>)

-   Video: Fixed recording is not created when path containing Japanese characters. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/errors-are-thrown-and-the-recording-file-is-not-created-when-japanese-characters-are-included-in-the-output-file-path" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116121</a>)

-   Video: Fixed settings not being applied when using the Preset Selector to apply a preset to a VideoClip. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/videoclipimporter-preset-not-applied-when-applying-preset-selector-on-already-imported-video-clips" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117137</a>)

-   Video: Fixed sporadic crash of video player on iOS when app is brought from background to foreground. (UUM-93765)

-   Video: Fixed: \[Windows\] Editor no longer crashes when listing webcams whose name is missing or empty. Such cameras are now skipped safely (with a warning in the Editor). (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/editor-crashes-with-platformwebcamtexture-getdevicenames-when-a-virtual-camera-is-present-and-friendlyname-property-is-deleted-from-registry" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-105563</a>)

-   Video: Re-importing a WebM file generates an inconsistent result (UUM-113154)

-   Video: Refine sRGB texture linearization compatibility checks for WebKit and older Firefox versions. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/transparent-webm-video-does-not-show-transparency-on-firefox-webgl-when-linear-color-space-is-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110367</a>)

-   Video: This release directly addresses several high-severity security vulnerabilities, identified by Black Duck scans as CVE-2023-6349, CVE-2024-5197, and CVE-2025-5283, when applicable, through targeted code changes, without upgrading the underlying libvpx library. Due to compatibility and integration constraints, an upgrade to a newer libvpx version was not possible; instead, alternative fixes have been implemented within the current version to mitigate these specific issues.

-   Video: VideoPlayer freezes for a short duration after 1 second when streaming on WebGL from StreamingAssets. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/videoplayer-freezes-for-a-short-duration-after-1-second-when-streaming-on-webgl-from-streamingassets" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-111608</a>)

-   Video: \[Android\] Fixed videoPlayer leaks memory when repeating Play and Stop on a Video. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/android-videoplayer-leaks-memory-when-repeating-play-and-stop-on-a-video" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-77668</a>)

-   Video: \[INSTABILITY\]: VideoPlayer_SeekAfterLoop_KeepsRendering (UUM-69542)

-   Video: \[Linux\] Crash on VideoPlayer::InvokeScriptCallbacks when domain reloads while playing a video. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/linux-crash-on-videoplayer-invokescriptcallbacks-when-domain-reloads-while-playing-a-video" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-110165</a>)

-   Video: \[WebGL\]\[iOS\]Erratic video playback when played on Chrome or Firefox (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/webgl-ios-erratic-video-playback-when-played-on-chrome-or-firefox" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-102147</a>)

-   VisionOS: Fixed crash when using WebCamTexture in visionOS builds.

-   VisionOS: Fixed type in platform define check. (UUM-111401)

-   VisionOS: Fixed Xcode build failure when advertising APIs are used from scripts. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/visionos-building-visionos-app-in-xcode-fails-with-error-adsupport-slash-asidentifiermanager-dot-h-file-not-found" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-113817</a>)

-   VisionOS: Fixed: A few audio app lifecycle issues were fixed on visionOS. Previously, when moving the Unity app through the various lifecycle states (i.e. foreground, background), audio could stop playing in error. (UUM-60646)

-   Web: Fixed a bug caused by the use of a deprecated windows utility. (UUM-104979)

-   Web: Fixed a crash happening when transform position carries NaN values. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/a-crash-occurs-on-a-webgl-build-when-setting-a-value-on-audioparam" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-74844</a>)

-   Web: Fixed an issue where WebGPU would not be passed the power preference settings from the WebGL player publishing settings. (UUM-114664)

-   Web: Fixed an issue where window events were being registered and handled before the engine fully initialized with WebGPU. (UUM-100658)

-   Web: Fixed an issue with texture compression that would cause webgpu errors and result in a black screen. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/building-the-project-for-web-with-astc-texture-compression-results-in-black-screen" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-97275</a>)

-   Web: Fixed code optimization options "Runtime Speed" and "Disk Size": disable Link Time Optimization(LTO).  
    This allows to workaround "Runtime Error: unreachable" bug with certain projects. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/runtime-error-unreachable-during-initialization-when-building-the-project-for-webgl-with-code-optimization" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-114084</a>)

-   Web: Fixed multithreading bug when UI Toolkit is used in multithreaded Web builds. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/argumentoutofrangeexception-and-uitk-is-rendered-incorrectly-when-building-in-webgl-with-native-c-slash-c-plus-plus-multithreading-enabled" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-108928</a>)

-   Web: Fixed Object.InstantiateAsync in multithreaded Web builds. (UUM-103893)

-   WebGL: Fixed a WebGL error with URP and MSAA, when a depth only render pass is involved. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/scene-is-not-rendered-and-renderpass-errors-when-urp-msaa-is-enabled-in-the-webgl-player" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-90459</a>)

-   WebGL: Fixed an issue where native and javascript AudioSource paused states would desynchronize during scene loading when Play On Awake is used and the AudioListener is paused. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/audiolistener-dot-pause-is-ignored-when-a-new-scene-is-loaded" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-96644</a>)

-   WebGL: Fixed an issue where resizing the WebGL canvas would cause flickering. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/webgl-player-flickers-and-logs-warnings-when-viewport-is-resized-and-linear-color-space-is-used" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-30810</a>)

-   WebGL: Fixed an issue where `DownloadHandlerScript.CompleteContent` was called twice in WebGL builds. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/downloadhandlerscript-dot-completecontent-is-called-twice-when-building-for-webgl" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-109862</a>)

-   WebGL: Fixed build warning from APV when targeting WebGPU. (UUM-117216)

-   WebGL: Fixed for WebGPU errors when using APV Lighting Scenario Blending. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/webgpu-errors-with-apv-lighting-scenario-blending" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117230</a>)

-   WebGL: Fixed regression that caused URP debug material overrides to fail for WebGL. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/webgl-material-override-does-not-apply-when-using-the-debugger-to-apply-it-in-a-webgl-build" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-112052</a>)

-   WebGL: Fixed WebGPU depthBiasSlopeScale error when rendering points. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/webgpu-errors-when-point-primitives-are-rendered" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116433</a>)

-   WebGL: Fixed WebGPU error when uploading compressed texture mip levels that aren't block size aligned. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/webgpu-errors-when-compressed-texture-has-non-aligned-mip-levels" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-116437</a>)

-   WebGL: WebGPU: Fixed compressed 3d textures if the WebGPU extension is available. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/webgpu-compressed-3d-textures-fail-even-when-extension-is-available" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-121604</a>)

-   WebGL: \[WebGPU\] Enable support for the float32-blendable extension to enable blending of RGBA32Float textures. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/32bit-floating-point-rendertexture-blending-feature-is-not-available-for-webgpu" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-117370</a>)

-   Windows: Fixed SystemInfo.deviceType to correctly return the type of device the Windows Standalone player is running on. (UUM-110392)

-   Windows: Updating gameloop when the user right-clicks on the window title bar on a Run In Background application. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/main-thread-freezes-when-right-clicking-on-the-title-bar-of-a-windowed-player-on-windows" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-124735</a>)

-   Windows: Updating PIX runtime libraries and fixing missing PIX markers for Unity profiling and graphics events. (UUM-110778)

-   XR: Fixed an issue where deployed URP Quest Applications threw Vulkan Errors on App Exit. (UUM-109083)

-   XR: Fixed an issue where the `XrDisplaySubsystem` tried to destroy textures that were still in use by other processes. This fix uses `FinishRendering()` to block the simulation thread until those resources are no longer in use before trying to destroy them. (UUM-109086)

-   XR: Fixed XR stats from gfx thread, like refreshRate being dropped issue. (<a href="https://issuetracker.unity3d.com/issues/xrdevice-dot-refreshrate-returns-0-dot-0-hz-when-running-standalone-build-in-vr-mode" class="underline hover:no-underline">UUM-104824</a>)

-   XR: Meta Quest now set to use Mobile quality as a default Quality Setting. This prevents SSAO from being enabled in a Meta Quest XR build, which has been shown to degrade runtime performance

-   XR: Updated the XR Hands package (`com.unity.xr.hands`) to version 1.6.0-pre.3.

-   XR: Updated XR Hands (com.unity.xr.hands) to version 1.6.0.
